    s.configure("Gold.TButton", background=COLORS["gold"], foreground="white",
                font=(FONT,10,"bold"), borderwidth=0)
    s.map("Gold.TButton", background=[("active",COLORS["gold_deep"])])
    s.configure("Green.TButton", background=COLORS["green"], foreground="white",
                font=(FONT,10,"bold"), borderwidth=0)
    s.map("Green.TButton", background=[("active",COLORS["green_deep"]),("disabled","#a0aab8")])
    s.configure("Treeview", font=(FONT,10), rowheight=28,
                background="white", fieldbackground="white", borderwidth=0)
    s.configure("Treeview.Heading", font=(FONT,10,"bold"),
                background=COLORS["navy"], foreground="white", padding=(6,6), borderwidth=0)
    s.map("Treeview.Heading", background=[("active",COLORS["navy_soft"])])
    s.map("Treeview", background=[("selected",COLORS["navy_soft"])],
                       foreground=[("selected","white")])
    s.configure("TNotebook", background=COLORS["bg"], borderwidth=0)
    s.configure("TNotebook.Tab", font=(FONT,10,"bold"), padding=(14,8),
                background="#e0e5ee", foreground=COLORS["gray_dark"], borderwidth=0)
    s.map("TNotebook.Tab", background=[("selected",COLORS["navy"])],
                          foreground=[("selected","white")])
    s.configure("TEntry", padding=5)
    s.configure("TCombobox", padding=5)
    s.configure("Horizontal.TProgressbar", background=COLORS["green"],
                troughcolor="#d1d5db", borderwidth=0, thickness=8)

# ============================================================
# TOAST
# ============================================================
class Toast(tk.Toplevel):
    _active=[]
    def __init__(self, parent, message, kind="info", duration=3000):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.overrideredirect(True)
        self.attributes("-topmost", True)
        palette={"info":COLORS["navy"],"success":COLORS["green"],
                 "warning":COLORS["gold_deep"],"error":COLORS["red"]}
        icons={"info":"ℹ","success":"✓","warning":"⚠","error":"✕"}
        bg=palette.get(kind, COLORS["navy"])
        frame=tk.Frame(self, bg=bg, padx=14, pady=10,
                       highlightthickness=1, highlightbackground="#cfd4dd")
        frame.pack()
        tk.Label(frame, text=icons.get(kind,""), bg=bg, fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,13,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=(0,10))
        tk.Label(frame, text=message, bg=bg, fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,10), justify="left").pack(side="left")
        self.update_idletasks()
        w=self.winfo_width(); h=self.winfo_height()
        try:
            px=parent.winfo_rootx()+parent.winfo_width()-w-24
            py=parent.winfo_rooty()+parent.winfo_height()-h-50
        except tk.TclError:
            px, py = 100, 100
        for t in list(Toast._active):
            try: py -= t.winfo_height() + 8
            except tk.TclError: pass
        self.geometry(f"+{max(0,px)}+{max(0,py)}")
        Toast._active.append(self)
        self.after(duration, self._close)
        self.bind("<Button-1>", lambda e: self._close())

    def _close(self):
        try:
            Toast._active.remove(self); self.destroy()
        except (ValueError, tk.TclError): pass


def _check_semantica_labels(data):
    """Verifica se cards de Volumetria vão inferir uma semântica razoável.

    Detecta 3 casos:
      1) semantica com valor inválido (ex: 'foo') → HTML cai em 'auto'
      2) semantica = 'auto' + rótulo que não casa com nenhum padrão
      3) semantica explícita e válida → sem pendência

    Cards com rótulo ambíguo caem em 'crescimento' por padrão — o que
    pode inverter as cores quando o card for de orçamento/gasto.
    """
    pendencias = []

    def _check(item):
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            return
        tipo = (item.get("type") or item.get("tipo") or "").strip().lower()
        if tipo not in ("yoy", "budget"):
            return

        lbl = (item.get("label") or "").strip()
        sem = (item.get("semantica") or "auto").strip().lower()

        # ── Caso 1: valor inválido (usuário digitou no JSON manualmente) ──
        if sem not in SEMANTICAS_VALIDAS:
            pendencias.append(
                f"Volumetria — '{lbl}': semântica '{sem}' desconhecida. "
                f"Valores válidos: {', '.join(SEMANTICAS_VALIDAS)}. "
                f"O HTML vai cair em 'auto' e inferir do rótulo."
            )
            return

        # ── Caso 3: usuário definiu explicitamente → OK ──
        if sem != "auto":
            return

        # ── Caso 2: auto + verifica se algum padrão casa com o rótulo ──
        for pattern, _ in _SEMANTICA_PATTERNS:
            if re.search(pattern, lbl, re.I):
                return  # inferência vai acertar

        # Não casou → cai no fallback da engine (que é 'crescimento')
        inferida = _inferir_semantica(lbl)
        pendencias.append(
            f"Volumetria — '{lbl}': rótulo não casa com nenhuma regra de "
            f"inferência. O card vai usar '{inferida}' por padrão "
            f"(positivo = 🟢, negativo = 🔴). Se for um card de orçamento, "
            f"custo ou tempo, defina a 'Semântica' explicitamente."
        )

    for c in _as_list(data.get("volumetria")):
        _check(c)

    return pendencias

# ============================================================
# VALIDATOR
# ============================================================
class Validator:
    TAB_ORDER = ["config", "descricoes", "volumetria", "estrategicos",
                 "taticos", "operacionais", "exportacao", "sac", "calculados"]

    def __init__(self, data): self.data=_as_dict(data)

    def analyze(self):
        d=self.data; errors=[]; pend=[]

        config_errors=[]
        p=str(d.get("periodo") or "").strip()
        a=str(d.get("atualizacao") or "").strip()
        if not p: config_errors.append("Configurações: campo 'Período' vazio")
        elif not periodo_valido(p):
            config_errors.append(f"Configurações: 'Período' inválido ('{p}'). Esperado: Mmm/AAAA")
        if not a: config_errors.append("Configurações: campo 'Atualização' vazio")
        elif not atualizacao_valida(a):
            config_errors.append(f"Configurações: 'Atualização' inválida ('{a}'). Esperado: DD/MM/AAAA HH:MM")
        errors.extend(config_errors)

        descricoes   = _as_list(d.get("descricoes"))
        volumetria   = _as_list(d.get("volumetria"))
        estrategicos = _as_list(d.get("estrategicos"))
        operacionais = _as_list(d.get("operacionais"))
        t   = _as_dict(d.get("taticos"))
        exp = _as_dict(d.get("exportacao"))
        sac = _as_dict(d.get("sac"))

        if not descricoes:   pend.append("Descrições: nenhum item (faixa rolante ficará vazia)")
        if not volumetria:   pend.append("Volumetria: nenhum card cadastrado")
        if not estrategicos: pend.append("Estratégicos: nenhum indicador")
        if not t.get("colunas"): pend.append("Táticos: nenhuma coluna")
        if not t.get("linhas"):  pend.append("Táticos: nenhuma linha")
        if not operacionais: pend.append("Operacionais: nenhuma seção (Página 2 vazia)")
        if not exp.get("kpis"): pend.append("Exportação: nenhum KPI")
        if not sac.get("kpis"): pend.append("SAC: nenhum KPI")

        panel_results = validate_all_panels(d)
        exp_panel_errors, exp_panel_warnings = panel_results["exportacao"]
        sac_panel_errors, sac_panel_warnings = panel_results["sac"]

        for e in exp_panel_errors:
            errors.append(f"Exportação: {e}")
        for w in exp_panel_warnings:
            pend.append(f"Exportação: {w}")

        sac_paineis = _as_list(sac.get("paineisDestaque"))
        sac_errors = list(sac_panel_errors)
        if not sac_paineis:
            pend.append("SAC: nenhum painel de destaque (gráficos de SAC não serão exibidos)")
        for e in sac_errors:
            errors.append(f"SAC: {e}")
        for w in sac_panel_warnings:
            pend.append(f"SAC: {w}")

        tempo_medio_errors = []
        for sec in operacionais:
            sec_titulo = (sec.get("titulo") or "seção sem título").strip()
            for idx, it in enumerate(_as_list(sec.get("itens"))):
                if not isinstance(it, dict):
                    continue
                if it.get("tipo_card") != "tempo_medio":
                    continue

                un  = it.get("unidade_tempo") or "dias"
                v   = _read_values(it)
                mt  = parse_tempo_value(v.get("meta"),     un)
                tm  = parse_tempo_value(v.get("tempoMes"), un)
                lbl = (it.get("label") or f"item #{idx+1}").strip()

                if mt is None or mt <= 0:
                    msg = (f"Operacionais/Tempo Médio — '{lbl}': "
                           f"meta inválida (esperado > 0 na unidade '{un}', "
                           f"recebeu '{v.get('meta','')}')")
                    errors.append(msg)
                    tempo_medio_errors.append(msg)

                if tm is None:
                    pend.append(
                        f"Operacionais/Tempo Médio — '{lbl}': "
                        f"tempo médio do mês vazio — o card ficará sem número em destaque"
                    )

                cons = _as_dict(it.get("consolidacao"))
                tipo_cons = cons.get("tipo", "soma")
                if it.get("pais") == "ambos" and tipo_cons == "soma":
                    pend.append(
                        f"Operacionais/Tempo Médio — '{lbl}': "
                        f"consolidação 'soma' não faz sentido para tempo médio. "
                        f"Use 'br_only', 'py_only' ou 'formula' (média ponderada por volume)."
                    )

                if un not in TEMPO_UNIDADES:
                    errors.append(
                        f"Operacionais/Tempo Médio — '{lbl}': "
                        f"unidade '{un}' desconhecida (aceitas: {', '.join(TEMPO_UNIDADES)})"
                    )

                valores = _as_dict(it.get("valores"))
                tem_br = bool(_as_dict(valores.get("BR")))
                tem_py = bool(_as_dict(valores.get("PY")))
                if it.get("pais") == "ambos" and not (tem_br and tem_py):
                    pend.append(
                        f"Operacionais/Tempo Médio — '{lbl}': "
                        f"card marcado como 'ambos' mas falta dados em "
                        f"{'BR' if not tem_br else ''}"
                        f"{' e ' if (not tem_br and not tem_py) else ''}"
                        f"{'PY' if not tem_py else ''}."
                    )

        operacionais_status = "warn" if operacionais else "warn"
        if operacionais:
            operacionais_status = "error" if tempo_medio_errors else "ok"

        calcs = _as_list(d.get("calculados"))
        calc_errors=[]
        if calcs:
            reg = build_formula_registry(d)
            for c in calcs:
                lbl = (c.get("label") or "").strip()
                if not lbl:
                    calc_errors.append("Calculados: existe card sem rótulo")
                    continue
                _, err = Formula.evaluate(c.get("formula",""), reg)
                if err: calc_errors.append(f"Calculados — '{lbl}': {err}")
        else:
            pend.append("Calculados: nenhum card com fórmula")
        errors.extend(calc_errors)

        # ⭐ NOVO — reporta itens operacionais vazios (que serão
        # removidos do HTML ao publicar)
        empty_items = []
        for sec in operacionais:
            sec_t = sec.get("titulo") or "(seção sem título)"
            for it in _as_list(sec.get("itens")):
                if not _op_item_has_data(it):
                    lbl = it.get("label") or "(sem rótulo)"
                    empty_items.append(f"{sec_t} → {lbl}")
        if empty_items:
            pend.append(
                f"{len(empty_items)} item(ns) operacional(is) sem dados "
                f"serão removidos ao publicar. Use '🧹 Limpar vazios' "
                f"na aba Operacionais para removê-los agora."
            )

        sem_calculo = [(sec, label) for sec,label,tier,_ in Insights.scan(d) if tier=="gray"]
        if sem_calculo:
            pend.append(f"{len(sem_calculo)} indicador(es) com meta vazia/zero/'-' — gauge ficará sem %")

        # ⭐ NOVO — verifica consistência semântica dos cards de Volumetria
        # (rótulo ambíguo sem 'semantica' explícita → pode pintar cor invertida)
        pend.extend(_check_semantica_labels(d))

        status={
            "config":       "error" if config_errors else "ok",
            "descricoes":   "ok" if descricoes else "warn",
            "volumetria":   "ok" if volumetria else "warn",
            "estrategicos": "ok" if estrategicos else "warn",
            "taticos":      "ok" if (t.get("colunas") and t.get("linhas")) else "warn",
            "operacionais": operacionais_status,
            "exportacao":   "error" if exp_panel_errors else ("ok" if exp.get("kpis") else "warn"),
            "sac":          "error" if sac_errors else ("ok" if (sac.get("kpis") and sac_paineis) else "warn"),
            "calculados":   "error" if calc_errors else ("ok" if calcs else "warn"),
        }
        return {"status":status,"errors":errors,"pendencies":pend}

# ============================================================
# RESPONSIVIDADE — SCROLL, TREE, JANELAS
# ============================================================
class ScrollableFrame(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent, bg=None, **kwargs):
        _bg = bg or COLORS["bg"]
        super().__init__(parent, bg=_bg, **kwargs)

        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(self, bg=_bg, highlightthickness=0, bd=0)
        self.vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="vertical",
                                  command=self.canvas.yview)
        self.canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=self.vsb.set)

        self.vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        self.canvas.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)

        self.inner = tk.Frame(self.canvas, bg=_bg)
        self._win_id = self.canvas.create_window(
            (0, 0), window=self.inner, anchor="nw"
        )

        self.inner.bind("<Configure>", self._on_inner_config)
        self.canvas.bind("<Configure>", self._on_canvas_config)
        self.canvas.bind("<Enter>", self._bind_wheel)
        self.canvas.bind("<Leave>", self._unbind_wheel)

    def _on_inner_config(self, _e=None):
        try:
            self.canvas.configure(scrollregion=self.canvas.bbox("all"))
        except tk.TclError:
            pass

    def _on_canvas_config(self, event):
        try:
            self.canvas.itemconfigure(self._win_id, width=event.width)
        except tk.TclError:
            pass

    def _bind_wheel(self, _e=None):
        self.canvas.bind_all("<MouseWheel>", self._on_wheel)
        self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-4>", self._on_wheel)
        self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-5>", self._on_wheel)

    def _unbind_wheel(self, _e=None):
        self.canvas.unbind_all("<MouseWheel>")
        self.canvas.unbind_all("<Button-4>")
        self.canvas.unbind_all("<Button-5>")

    def _on_wheel(self, event):
        try:
            if getattr(event, "num", None) == 4:
                self.canvas.yview_scroll(-1, "units")
            elif getattr(event, "num", None) == 5:
                self.canvas.yview_scroll(1, "units")
            else:
                delta = -1 if event.delta > 0 else 1
                self.canvas.yview_scroll(delta, "units")
        except tk.TclError:
            pass


def make_scrollable_body(parent, bg=None, padding=None):
    _bg = bg or COLORS["bg"]
    sf = ScrollableFrame(parent, bg=_bg)
    sf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
    if padding:
        pad_frame = tk.Frame(sf.inner, bg=_bg)
        pad_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                        padx=padding[0], pady=padding[1])
        return pad_frame
    return sf.inner


class AutoScrollTree(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent, columns, height=10,
                 selectmode="extended", show="headings", bg=None):
        _bg = bg or COLORS["card"]
        super().__init__(parent, bg=_bg)
        self.tree = ttk.Treeview(self, columns=[c[0] for c in columns],
                                  show=show, selectmode=selectmode, height=height)
        self._columns = columns
        self._initialized = False

    def build(self):
        for col in self._columns:
            self.tree.heading(col[0], text=col[1])
            self.tree.column(col[0], width=col[2],
                             anchor=col[3] if len(col) > 3 else "w",
                             stretch=True)
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="vertical", command=self.tree.yview)
        hsb = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="horizontal", command=self.tree.xview)
        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set, xscrollcommand=hsb.set)
        self.tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vsb.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hsb.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        self.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._initialized = True
        return self.tree


def enable_fullscreen(win):
    state = {"fs": False, "geom": None}

    def toggle(_e=None):
        try:
            state["fs"] = not state["fs"]
            if state["fs"]:
                state["geom"] = win.geometry()
                win.attributes("-fullscreen", True)
            else:
                win.attributes("-fullscreen", False)
                if state["geom"]:
                    win.geometry(state["geom"])
        except tk.TclError:
            pass
        return "break"

    win.bind("<F11>", toggle)


def enable_minimize_maximize(win, min_w=460, min_h=320, fit_to_content=True):
    try:
        win.resizable(True, True)
        win.minsize(min_w, min_h)
        try:
            win.attributes("-toolwindow", False)
        except tk.TclError:
            pass
        if fit_to_content:
            try:
                win.update_idletasks()
                screen_h = win.winfo_screenheight()
                screen_w = win.winfo_screenwidth()
                max_h = int(screen_h * 0.92)
                max_w = int(screen_w * 0.95)

                cur_w = win.winfo_width()
                if cur_w <= 1:
                    cur_w = win.winfo_reqwidth()
                cur_w = min(max(cur_w, min_w), max_w)

                cur_h = win.winfo_height()
                if cur_h <= 1:
                    cur_h = win.winfo_reqheight()
                req_h = win.winfo_reqheight()
                target_h = max(cur_h, min(req_h, max_h))
                target_h = max(target_h, min_h)

                win.geometry(f"{cur_w}x{target_h}")
            except tk.TclError:
                pass
    except tk.TclError:
        pass


def fit_window_to_screen(win, default_w, default_h, parent=None,
                         min_w=520, min_h=380, margin=0.92):
    try:
        win.update_idletasks()
        screen_w = win.winfo_screenwidth()
        screen_h = win.winfo_screenheight()
        max_w = int(screen_w * margin)
        max_h = int(screen_h * margin)

        w = min(default_w, max_w)
        h_req = win.winfo_reqheight()
        h = min(max(default_h, h_req + 20), max_h)
        h = max(h, min_h)

        if parent is not None:
            try:
                px = parent.winfo_rootx() + (parent.winfo_width() - w) // 2
                py = parent.winfo_rooty() + (parent.winfo_height() - h) // 2
            except tk.TclError:
                px = (screen_w - w) // 2
                py = (screen_h - h) // 2
        else:
            px = (screen_w - w) // 2
            py = (screen_h - h) // 2

        px = max(0, min(px, screen_w - w))
        py = max(0, min(py, screen_h - h))
        win.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{px}+{py}")
    except tk.TclError:
        pass


def _pack_botoes_no_fundo(toplevel, btns_frame, padx=16, pady=(0, 14)):
    btns_frame.pack(fill="x", side="bottom", padx=padx, pady=pady)


# ============================================================
# FILTERABLE TREE (com scroll horizontal automático)
# ============================================================
class FilterableTree(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent, columns, on_double=None, show_search=True):
        super().__init__(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        if show_search:
            sf=tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"]); sf.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
            tk.Label(sf, text="🔍", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                     font=(FONT,11)).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
            self.search_var=tk.StringVar()
            self.search_var.trace_add("write", lambda *a: self._apply())
            ttk.Entry(sf, textvariable=self.search_var).pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            self.lbl_count=tk.Label(sf, text="", bg=COLORS["bg"],
                                    fg=COLORS["text_gray"], font=(FONT,8,"italic"))
            self.lbl_count.pack(side="left", padx=(8,0))
        else:
            self.search_var=None

        cont=tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                      highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
        cont.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        self.tree=ttk.Treeview(cont, columns=[c[0] for c in columns],
                               show="headings", selectmode="extended")
        for col in columns:
            self.tree.heading(col[0], text=col[1])
            self.tree.column(col[0], width=col[2],
                             anchor=col[3] if len(col)>3 else "w", stretch=True)

        vsb=ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="vertical", command=self.tree.yview)
        hsb=ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="horizontal", command=self.tree.xview)
        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set, xscrollcommand=hsb.set)

        self.tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vsb.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hsb.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        cont.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
        cont.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        self._rows=[]
        if on_double: self.tree.bind("<Double-1>", on_double)

    def set_rows(self, rows):
        self._rows=list(rows); self._apply()

    def _apply(self):
        q=(self.search_var.get().strip().lower() if self.search_var else "")
        self.tree.delete(*self.tree.get_children())
        n=0
        for iid, values in self._rows:
            if not q or q in " ".join(str(v) for v in values).lower():
                self.tree.insert("","end",iid=iid, values=values); n+=1
        if self.search_var:
            total=len(self._rows)
            self.lbl_count.config(text=f"({n}/{total})" if q else f"({total} itens)")

    def selection(self): return self.tree.selection()
    def clear(self): self.tree.delete(*self.tree.get_children())

# ============================================================
# HELPERS UI
# ============================================================
def toolbar(parent, buttons):
    bar=tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,8))
    for b in buttons:
        text=b[0]; style=b[1] if len(b)>1 else "TButton"; cmd=b[2] if len(b)>2 else None
        ttk.Button(bar, text=text, style=style, command=cmd).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
    return bar


def make_card(parent, title=None):
    card=tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                  highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
    card.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(0,8))
    if title:
        tk.Label(card, text=title, bg=COLORS["card"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
                 font=(FONT,11,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", padx=14, pady=(10,4))
        tk.Frame(card, bg=COLORS["border"], height=1).pack(fill="x", padx=14, pady=(0,8))
    return card

# ============================================================
# TRAVA DE RÓTULOS ÚNICOS
# ============================================================
def find_label_collision(data, label, ignore_path=None):
    label = (label or "").strip()
    if not label: return None

    def _ignored(*path):
        return ignore_path == path

    for i, c in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("volumetria"))):
        if _ignored("volumetria", i): continue
        if (c.get("label") or "").strip() == label:
            return f"Volumetria → #{i+1} (tipo '{c.get('type','?')}')"

    for i, e in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("estrategicos"))):
        if _ignored("estrategicos", i): continue
        if (e.get("titulo") or "").strip() == label:
            return f"Estratégicos → #{i+1}"

    for si, sec in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("operacionais"))):
        for ii, it in enumerate(_as_list(sec.get("itens"))):
            if _ignored("operacionais", si, ii): continue
            if (it.get("label") or "").strip() == label:
                sec_t = sec.get("titulo") or f"seção {si+1}"
                return f"Operacionais → {sec_t} → #{ii+1}"

    t = _as_dict(data.get("taticos")); cols = _as_list(t.get("colunas"))
    for ri, row in enumerate(_as_list(t.get("linhas"))):
        if _ignored("taticos", ri): continue
        if (row.get("label") or "").strip() == label:
            return f"Táticos → linha #{ri+1}"
        metrics = _read_values(row).get("metrics", row.get("metrics", []))
        for mi, m in enumerate(_as_list(metrics)):
            if _ignored("taticos", ri, mi): continue
            if not isinstance(m, dict): continue
            col = cols[mi] if mi < len(cols) else f"m{mi+1}"
            full = f"{row.get('label','')} — {col}"
            if full == label:
                return f"Táticos → linha #{ri+1} → {col}"

    for i, c in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("calculados"))):
        if _ignored("calculados", i): continue
        if (c.get("label") or "").strip() == label:
            return f"Calculados → #{i+1}"

    for sec_key, sec_label in (("exportacao", "Exportação"), ("sac", "SAC")):
        sec = _as_dict(data.get(sec_key))
        for i, p in enumerate(_as_list(sec.get("paineisDestaque"))):
            if _ignored(sec_key, "paineisDestaque", i): continue
            if (p.get("titulo") or "").strip() == label:
                return f"{sec_label} → Painel #{i+1}"

    return None


def find_all_duplicate_labels(data):
    seen = {}

    def _add(label, where):
        label = (label or "").strip()
        if not label: return
        seen.setdefault(label, []).append(where)

    for i, c in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("volumetria"))):
        _add(c.get("label"), f"Volumetria #{i+1}")
    for i, e in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("estrategicos"))):
        _add(e.get("titulo"), f"Estratégicos #{i+1}")
    for si, sec in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("operacionais"))):
        sec_t = sec.get("titulo") or f"seção {si+1}"
        for ii, it in enumerate(_as_list(sec.get("itens"))):
            _add(it.get("label"), f"Operacionais/{sec_t} #{ii+1}")

    t = _as_dict(data.get("taticos")); cols = _as_list(t.get("colunas"))
    for ri, row in enumerate(_as_list(t.get("linhas"))):
        metrics = _read_values(row).get("metrics", row.get("metrics", []))
        for mi, m in enumerate(_as_list(metrics)):
            if not isinstance(m, dict): continue
            col = cols[mi] if mi < len(cols) else f"m{mi+1}"
            _add(f"{row.get('label','')} — {col}",
                 f"Táticos/linha {ri+1}/{col}")

    for i, c in enumerate(_as_list(data.get("calculados"))):
        _add(c.get("label"), f"Calculados #{i+1}")

    for sec_key, sec_label in (("exportacao", "Exportação"), ("sac", "SAC")):
        sec = _as_dict(data.get(sec_key))
        for i, p in enumerate(_as_list(sec.get("paineisDestaque"))):
            _add(p.get("titulo"), f"{sec_label} #painel {i+1}")

    return [(lbl, locs) for lbl, locs in seen.items() if len(locs) > 1]

# ============================================================
# YoY CALC DIALOG
# ============================================================
class YoYCalcDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, target_var=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title("Calculadora de Variação (YoY)")
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"])
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()
        self.target_var = target_var
        self._result = None

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=18, pady=(0, 14))
        if target_var is not None:
            ttk.Button(btns, text="✓ Usar este valor",
                       style="Primary.TButton",
                       command=self._use).pack(side="right", padx=(6, 0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Fechar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44)
        hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text="Calculadora de Variação (YoY)",
                 bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT, 12, "bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        body.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=18, pady=14)
        body.columnconfigure(0, weight=1, uniform="col")
        body.columnconfigure(1, weight=1, uniform="col")

        tk.Label(body, text="Valor atual",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT, 9, "bold"), anchor="w"
                 ).grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 8), pady=(0, 2))
        tk.Label(body, text="Valor anterior",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT, 9, "bold"), anchor="w"
                 ).grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(8, 0), pady=(0, 2))

        self.v_atual = tk.StringVar()
        self.v_anterior = tk.StringVar()
        ttk.Entry(body, textvariable=self.v_atual).grid(
            row=1, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 8))
        ttk.Entry(body, textvariable=self.v_anterior).grid(
            row=1, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(8, 0))

        res_frame = tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        res_frame.grid(row=2, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(16, 4))
        res_frame.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.lbl_result = tk.Label(
            res_frame, text="—", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
            font=(FONT, 18, "bold"), anchor="center",
        )
        self.lbl_result.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")

        tk.Label(body, text="Fórmula:  (atual − anterior) ÷ anterior × 100",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                 font=(FONT, 8, "italic"), anchor="center"
                 ).grid(row=3, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(6, 0))

        self.v_atual.trace_add("write", self._calc)
        self.v_anterior.trace_add("write", self._calc)

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 460, 260, parent=parent, min_w=460, min_h=260)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 420, 240, fit_to_content=False)
        enable_fullscreen(self)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())

    def _calc(self, *_):
        delta = Calc.variacao(self.v_atual.get(), self.v_anterior.get())
        if delta is None:
            self.lbl_result.config(text="—", fg=COLORS["text_gray"])
            self._result = None
            return
        texto, cor = Calc.format_variacao(delta)
        self.lbl_result.config(text=texto, fg=cor)
        self._result = round(delta, 1)

    def _use(self):
        if self._result is not None and self.target_var is not None:
            self.target_var.set(str(self._result).replace(".", ","))
        self.destroy()

# ============================================================
# VALIDADORES POR NOME DE CAMPO
# ============================================================
FIELD_VALIDATORS = {
    "direction":  (["higher","lower",""], "Aceita apenas 'higher' ou 'lower'"),
    "color":      (["navy","red","gold","green",""], "Cor: navy, red, gold ou green"),
    "icon":       (["users","refresh","dollar","truck","calculator","headset",""],
                   "Ícone não reconhecido"),
    "type":       (["budget","yoy",""], "Tipo: 'budget' ou 'yoy'"),
    "mesStatus":  (["","green","yellow","red"], ""),
    "ytdStatus":  (["","green","yellow","red"], ""),
    "status":     (["","green","yellow","red"], ""),
    "chart1Type": (["vertical","horizontal","line","vertical-dual","vertical-stacked",""],
                   "Tipo de gráfico inválido"),
    "chart2Type": (["vertical","horizontal","line","vertical-dual","vertical-stacked",""],
                   "Tipo de gráfico inválido"),
    "chart1Color":(["var(--navy)","var(--red)","var(--gold-deep)","var(--yellow)",""],
                   "Cor CSS inválida"),
    "chart2Color":(["var(--navy)","var(--red)","var(--gold-deep)","var(--yellow)",""],
                   "Cor CSS inválida"),
    "format":     (["auto","decimal","integer","percent","percent_frac",
                    "currency_brl","currency_usd","compact","raw",""],
                   "Formato inválido"),

    # ⭐ NOVO — controla a cor das variações (Desvio Mês/YTD, YoY, vs Previsto)
    #    Precisa estar aqui para o JsonGridEditor destacar valores fora do padrão
    #    e para o combo sugerir as opções corretas no form.
    "semantica":  (["auto","economia","crescimento","reducao","neutro",""],
                   "Semântica: auto, economia, crescimento, reducao ou neutro"),
}
# ============================================================
# JSON GRID EDITOR
# ============================================================
class JsonGridEditor(tk.Frame):
    MAX_HISTORY    = 50
    DRAG_THRESHOLD = 5

    def __init__(self, parent, value=None, on_change=None, height=10,
                 highlight_empty=False):
        super().__init__(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        self._on_change = on_change
        self._height = height
        self._highlight_empty = highlight_empty
        self._value = self._normalize(value)
        self._tree = None
        self._table_cols = []
        self._inline_editor = None
        self._history = [deepcopy(self._value)]
        self._hist_idx = 0
        self._drag_row = None
        self._drag_start_y = None
        self._drag_active = False
        self._build()

    def _normalize(self, v):
        return {} if v is None else deepcopy(v)

    def get_value(self):
        return deepcopy(self._value)

    def set_value(self, v):
        norm = self._normalize(v)
        if norm == self._value:
            return
        self._push_history()
        self._value = norm
        self._rebuild()

    def _notify(self):
        if self._on_change:
            try: self._on_change(self.get_value())
            except Exception: logging.exception("on_change do JsonGridEditor falhou")

    def _rebuild(self):
        self._cancel_inline()
        for w in self.winfo_children(): w.destroy()
        self._build()

    def _push_history(self):
        snap = deepcopy(self._value)
        if self._hist_idx >= 0 and self._history[self._hist_idx] == snap:
            return
        if self._hist_idx < len(self._history) - 1:
            self._history = self._history[:self._hist_idx + 1]
        self._history.append(snap)
        self._hist_idx += 1
        if len(self._history) > self.MAX_HISTORY:
            self._history.pop(0); self._hist_idx -= 1

    def _undo(self, *_):
        if self._hist_idx <= 0:
            return "break"
        self._hist_idx -= 1
        self._value = deepcopy(self._history[self._hist_idx])
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()
        return "break"

    def _redo(self, *_):
        if self._hist_idx >= len(self._history) - 1:
            return "break"
        self._hist_idx += 1
        self._value = deepcopy(self._history[self._hist_idx])
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()
        return "break"

    def _build(self):
        v = self._value
        if isinstance(v, dict):    self._build_dict_view()
        elif isinstance(v, list):
            if not v or all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in v):
                self._build_table_view()
            else:
                self._build_list_view()
        else:                      self._build_scalar_view()

    def _format_value(self, v):
        if v is None: return "null"
        if isinstance(v, bool): return "true" if v else "false"
        if isinstance(v, dict): return f"{{ {len(v)} campo(s) }} ▸"
        if isinstance(v, list): return f"[ {len(v)} item(ns) ] ▸"
        return str(v)

    def _coerce(self, s, original=None):
        s = (s or "").strip()
        if s == "": return ""
        if isinstance(original, bool):
            low = s.lower()
            if low in ("true","1","sim","yes"): return True
            if low in ("false","0","não","nao","no"): return False
            return s
        if isinstance(original, int):
            try: return int(s)
            except ValueError: pass
        if isinstance(original, float):
            try: return float(s.replace(",", "."))
            except ValueError: pass
        if isinstance(original, str): return s
        if re.fullmatch(r"-?\d+", s):
            try: return int(s)
            except ValueError: pass
        if re.fullmatch(r"-?\d+\.\d+", s):
            try: return float(s)
            except ValueError: pass
        return s

    def _validates(self, field_name, value):
        if not field_name or field_name not in FIELD_VALIDATORS:
            return (True, "")
        allowed, msg = FIELD_VALIDATORS[field_name]
        if value == "" or str(value) in allowed:
            return (True, "")
        return (False, msg or f"Valor '{value}' não esperado para '{field_name}'")

    def _make_toolbar(self, parent):
        bar = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
        return bar

    def _add_history_buttons(self, bar):
        tk.Frame(bar, bg=COLORS["border"], width=1).pack(side="left", fill="y", padx=6)
        ttk.Button(bar, text="↶ Desfazer", command=self._undo, width=12).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="↷ Refazer",  command=self._redo, width=12).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))

    def _build_dict_view(self):
        bar = self._make_toolbar(self)
        ttk.Button(bar, text="➕ Campo", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._dict_add_field).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="🗑 Remover", style="Danger.TButton",
                   command=self._dict_del_field).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        self._add_history_buttons(bar)
        tk.Label(bar, text="(duplo-clique p/ editar · arraste p/ reordenar · ▸ = aninhado)",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                 font=(FONT,8,"italic")).pack(side="left", padx=(8,0))

        cont = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                        highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
        cont.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._tree = ttk.Treeview(cont, columns=("chave","valor"),
                                   show="headings", selectmode="browse",
                                   height=self._height)
        self._tree.heading("chave", text="Chave")
        self._tree.heading("valor", text="Valor")
        self._tree.column("chave", width=200, anchor="w", stretch=False)
        self._tree.column("valor", width=380, anchor="w", stretch=True)
        self._tree.tag_configure("invalid", background="#ffe2e2")
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="vertical", command=self._tree.yview)
        hsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="horizontal", command=self._tree.xview)
        self._tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set, xscrollcommand=hsb.set)
        self._tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vsb.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hsb.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        cont.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1); cont.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._bind_tree_events()
        self._refresh_dict()

    def _refresh_dict(self):
        self._tree.delete(*self._tree.get_children())
        for k, v in self._value.items():
            self._tree.insert("", "end", iid=k, values=(k, self._format_value(v)))
        self._tag_invalid_dict()

    def _dict_add_field(self):
        key = simpledialog.askstring("Nova chave", "Nome do campo:", parent=self)
        if not key: return
        if key in self._value:
            messagebox.showerror("Erro", f"Chave '{key}' já existe.", parent=self); return
        self._push_history()
        self._value[key] = ""
        self._refresh_dict(); self._notify()

    def _dict_del_field(self):
        s = self._tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        key = s[0]
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar", f"Remover campo '{key}'?", parent=self): return
        self._push_history()
        del self._value[key]
        self._refresh_dict(); self._notify()

    def _tag_invalid_dict(self):
        if self._tree is None or not isinstance(self._value, dict): return
        for key in self._value:
            if not self._tree.exists(key): continue
            ok, _ = self._validates(key, self._value[key])
            self._tree.item(key, tags=() if ok else ("invalid",))

    def _build_table_view(self):
        bar = self._make_toolbar(self)
        ttk.Button(bar, text="➕ Linha", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._table_add_row).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="📋 Duplicar",
                   command=self._table_dup_row).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="🗑 Remover", style="Danger.TButton",
                   command=self._table_del_row).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="🔧 Colunas",
                   command=self._table_edit_columns).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="🧹 Limpar vazias",
                   command=self._table_clear_empty_rows
                   ).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        self._add_history_buttons(bar)

        cols = []
        seen = set()
        for row in self._value:
            if not isinstance(row, dict): continue
            for k in row.keys():
                if k not in seen:
                    seen.add(k); cols.append(k)
        self._table_cols = cols

        cont = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                        highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
        cont.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        if not cols:
            tk.Label(cont, text="(sem colunas — clique em '🔧 Colunas' para definir)",
                     bg=COLORS["card"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                     font=(FONT,10,"italic")).pack(pady=20)
            self._tree = None
            return

        self._tree = ttk.Treeview(cont, columns=cols, show="headings",
                                   selectmode="extended", height=self._height)
        for c in cols:
            self._tree.heading(c, text=c)
            self._tree.column(c, width=max(90, 700//max(len(cols),1)),
                              anchor="w", stretch=True)
        self._tree.tag_configure("invalid", background="#ffe2e2")
        self._tree.tag_configure("empty",   background="#f2f4f8",
                                              foreground="#a0aab8")
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="vertical", command=self._tree.yview)
        hsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="horizontal", command=self._tree.xview)
        self._tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set, xscrollcommand=hsb.set)
        self._tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vsb.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hsb.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        cont.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1); cont.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._bind_tree_events()
        self._refresh_table()

    def _refresh_table(self):
        if self._tree is None: return
        self._tree.delete(*self._tree.get_children())
        for idx, row in enumerate(self._value):
            vals = [self._format_value(row.get(c) if isinstance(row, dict) else None)
                    for c in self._table_cols]
            tags = ()
            if self._highlight_empty and self._row_is_empty(row):
                tags = ("empty",)
            self._tree.insert("", "end", iid=str(idx), values=vals, tags=tags)
        self._tag_invalid_table()

    def _tag_invalid_table(self):
        if self._tree is None or not isinstance(self._value, list): return
        for idx, row in enumerate(self._value):
            iid = str(idx)
            if not self._tree.exists(iid) or not isinstance(row, dict): continue
            invalid = False
            for col in self._table_cols:
                ok, _ = self._validates(col, row.get(col))
                if not ok:
                    invalid = True; break
            self._tree.item(iid, tags=("invalid",) if invalid else ())

    def _table_add_row(self):
        self._push_history()
        self._value.append({c: "" for c in self._table_cols})
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _table_dup_row(self):
        if self._tree is None: return
        s = self._tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        self._push_history()
        for iid in sorted([int(x) for x in s], reverse=True):
            dup = deepcopy(self._value[iid]); self._value.insert(iid+1, dup)
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _table_del_row(self):
        if self._tree is None: return
        s = self._tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar", f"Remover {len(s)} linha(s)?", parent=self): return
        self._push_history()
        for iid in sorted([int(x) for x in s], reverse=True):
            del self._value[iid]
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _table_edit_columns(self):
        dlg = TableColumnsDialog(self, current=self._table_cols)
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result is None: return
        new_cols = dlg.result
        self._push_history()
        for row in self._value:
            if not isinstance(row, dict): continue
            for c in new_cols: row.setdefault(c, "")
            for k in list(row.keys()):
                if k not in new_cols: del row[k]
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _row_is_empty(self, row):
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            return not row
        for v in row.values():
            if v is None: continue
            if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() == "": continue
            return False
        return True

    def _table_clear_empty_rows(self):
        if not isinstance(self._value, list):
            return
        vazias = [i for i, r in enumerate(self._value) if self._row_is_empty(r)]
        if not vazias:
            messagebox.showinfo("Limpar vazias",
                "Nenhuma linha vazia encontrada.", parent=self)
            return
        if len(vazias) > 20:
            ok = messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar",
                f"Encontradas {len(vazias)} linha(s) vazia(s).\n\nRemover todas?",
                parent=self)
            if not ok: return
        self._push_history()
        self._value = [r for i, r in enumerate(self._value) if i not in vazias]
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _build_list_view(self):
        bar = self._make_toolbar(self)
        ttk.Button(bar, text="➕ Item", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._list_add).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="🗑 Remover", style="Danger.TButton",
                   command=self._list_del).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        self._add_history_buttons(bar)

        cont = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                        highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
        cont.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._tree = ttk.Treeview(cont, columns=("valor",), show="headings",
                                   selectmode="extended", height=self._height)
        self._tree.heading("valor", text="Valor")
        self._tree.column("valor", width=480, anchor="w", stretch=True)
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="vertical", command=self._tree.yview)
        hsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="horizontal", command=self._tree.xview)
        self._tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set, xscrollcommand=hsb.set)
        self._tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vsb.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hsb.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        cont.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1); cont.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._bind_tree_events()
        self._refresh_list()

    def _refresh_list(self):
        self._tree.delete(*self._tree.get_children())
        for idx, v in enumerate(self._value):
            self._tree.insert("", "end", iid=str(idx), values=(self._format_value(v),))

    def _list_add(self):
        self._push_history()
        self._value.append(""); self._refresh_list(); self._notify()

    def _list_del(self):
        s = self._tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar", "Remover item(ns)?", parent=self): return
        self._push_history()
        for iid in sorted([int(x) for x in s], reverse=True):
            del self._value[iid]
        self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _build_scalar_view(self):
        bar = self._make_toolbar(self)
        self._add_history_buttons(bar)
        tk.Label(self, text="Valor:", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(anchor="w")
        self._scalar_var = tk.StringVar(value="" if self._value is None else str(self._value))
        ttk.Entry(self, textvariable=self._scalar_var).pack(fill="x")
        def _on_change(*_):
            self._value = self._scalar_var.get()
            self._notify()
        self._scalar_var.trace_add("write", _on_change)

    def _bind_tree_events(self):
        self._tree.bind("<Double-1>",        self._on_double_click)
        self._tree.bind("<ButtonPress-1>",   self._on_drag_start)
        self._tree.bind("<B1-Motion>",       self._on_drag_motion)
        self._tree.bind("<ButtonRelease-1>", self._on_drag_release)
        self._tree.bind("<Control-z>",       self._undo)
        self._tree.bind("<Control-y>",       self._redo)
        self._tree.bind("<Control-Shift-Z>", self._redo)

    def _on_drag_start(self, event):
        region = self._tree.identify("region", event.x, event.y)
        if region != "cell":
            self._drag_row = None; return
        self._drag_row = self._tree.identify_row(event.y)
        self._drag_start_y = event.y
        self._drag_active = False

    def _on_drag_motion(self, event):
        if not self._drag_row: return
        if not self._drag_active:
            if self._drag_start_y is None: return
            if abs(event.y - self._drag_start_y) < self.DRAG_THRESHOLD: return
            self._drag_active = True
            try: self._tree.config(cursor="fleur")
            except tk.TclError: pass
        target = self._tree.identify_row(event.y)
        if not target or target == self._drag_row: return
        try: self._tree.move(self._drag_row, "", self._tree.index(target))
        except tk.TclError: pass

    def _on_drag_release(self, event):
        was_active = self._drag_active
        drag_row = self._drag_row
        self._drag_active = False
        self._drag_row = None
        self._drag_start_y = None
        try: self._tree.config(cursor="")
        except tk.TclError: pass
        if not was_active or not drag_row: return
        self._apply_drag_order()

    def _apply_drag_order(self):
        new_order = list(self._tree.get_children())
        v = self._value
        if isinstance(v, list) and all(iid.isdigit() for iid in new_order):
            try: reordered = [v[int(iid)] for iid in new_order]
            except (IndexError, ValueError): return
            if reordered == v: return
            self._push_history()
            self._value = reordered
            self._rebuild(); self._notify()
        elif isinstance(v, dict):
            try:
                reordered = {k: v[k] for k in new_order if k in v}
                for k in v:
                    if k not in reordered: reordered[k] = v[k]
            except Exception: return
            if reordered == v: return
            self._push_history()
            self._value = reordered
            self._rebuild(); self._notify()

    def _cancel_inline(self):
        if self._inline_editor:
            try: self._inline_editor.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._inline_editor = None

    def _on_double_click(self, event):
        region = self._tree.identify("region", event.x, event.y)
        if region != "cell": return
        row = self._tree.identify_row(event.y)
        col = self._tree.identify_column(event.x)
        if not row: return
        col_idx = int(col.replace("#", "")) - 1

        v = self._value
        if isinstance(v, dict):
            original = v.get(row)
            if isinstance(original, (dict, list)):
                self._open_sub_editor(row, original); return
            self._start_inline(row, col, original,
                lambda new: self._commit_dict(row, new),
                field_name=row)
        elif isinstance(v, list) and v and isinstance(v[0], dict):
            row_idx = int(row)
            col_name = self._table_cols[col_idx]
            original = v[row_idx].get(col_name)
            if isinstance(original, (dict, list)):
                self._open_sub_editor_at(row_idx, col_name, original); return
            self._start_inline(row, col, original,
                lambda new: self._commit_table(row_idx, col_name, new),
                field_name=col_name)
        elif isinstance(v, list):
            row_idx = int(row)
            original = v[row_idx]
            if isinstance(original, (dict, list)):
                self._open_sub_editor_at(row_idx, None, original); return
            self._start_inline(row, col, original,
                lambda new: self._commit_list(row_idx, new),
                field_name=None)

    def _start_inline(self, row_iid, col_id, original, commit, field_name=None):
        try: x, y, w, h = self._tree.bbox(row_iid, col_id)
        except Exception: return
        if w == 0: return
        self._cancel_inline()

        allowed = None; msg = ""
        if field_name and field_name in FIELD_VALIDATORS:
            allowed, msg = FIELD_VALIDATORS[field_name]

        if allowed is not None:
            editor = ttk.Combobox(self._tree, values=allowed, state="normal")
        else:
            editor = ttk.Entry(self._tree)

        editor.place(x=x, y=y, width=w, height=h)
        editor.insert(0, "" if original is None else str(original))
        try: editor.select_range(0, "end")
        except Exception: pass
        editor.focus_set()
        self._inline_editor = editor
        done = [False]

        def do_commit(_e=None):
            if done[0]: return
            done[0] = True
            raw = editor.get()
            new_val = self._coerce(raw, original)
            if allowed is not None and str(new_val) not in allowed:
                keep = messagebox.askyesno(
                    "Valor fora do padrão",
                    f"'{new_val}' não é um valor esperado para '{field_name}'.\n"
                    f"{msg}\n\nUsar mesmo assim?",
                    parent=self)
                if not keep:
                    try: editor.destroy()
                    except Exception: pass
                    self._inline_editor = None
                    return
            try: editor.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._inline_editor = None
            commit(new_val)

        def do_cancel(_e=None):
            if done[0]: return
            done[0] = True
            try: editor.destroy()
            except Exception: pass
            self._inline_editor = None

        editor.bind("<Return>",     do_commit)
        editor.bind("<KP_Enter>",   do_commit)
        editor.bind("<FocusOut>",   do_commit)
        editor.bind("<Escape>",     do_cancel)
        if isinstance(editor, ttk.Combobox):
            editor.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", do_commit)

    def _commit_dict(self, key, new_val):
        self._push_history()
        self._value[key] = new_val
        self._refresh_dict(); self._notify()

    def _commit_table(self, row_idx, col_name, new_val):
        try: self._value[row_idx][col_name] = new_val
        except (IndexError, KeyError): return
        self._push_history()
        self._refresh_table(); self._notify()

    def _commit_list(self, row_idx, new_val):
        try: self._value[row_idx] = new_val
        except IndexError: return
        self._push_history()
        self._refresh_list(); self._notify()

    def _open_sub_editor(self, key, sub_value):
        dlg = JsonEditorDialog(self, title=f"Editar '{key}'", value=sub_value)
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result is not None:
            self._push_history()
            self._value[key] = dlg.result
            self._refresh_dict(); self._notify()

    def _open_sub_editor_at(self, row_idx, col_name, sub_value):
        label = f"item {row_idx}" if col_name is None else f"item {row_idx} → {col_name}"
        dlg = JsonEditorDialog(self, title=f"Editar {label}", value=sub_value)
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result is not None:
            self._push_history()
            if col_name is None: self._value[row_idx] = dlg.result
            else: self._value[row_idx][col_name] = dlg.result
            self._rebuild(); self._notify()

# ============================================================
# CARD PICKER
# ============================================================
class CardPickerDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, cards):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title("Inserir referência na fórmula")
        self.result = None
        self._cards = cards
        self._payload = {}
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); self.transient(parent); self.grab_set()

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(0, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="✓ Inserir", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._pick).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44); hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text="O que você quer inserir na fórmula?",
                 bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"]); body.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                                                            padx=16, pady=14)
        tk.Label(body,
                 text="🔖 = valor principal do CARD    •    ·  = campo específico (ex.: [Card.ano2026.mes])",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,6))

        tree = FilterableTree(body, [
            ("ref",   "O que será inserido", 520),
            ("valor", "Valor atual",         180),
            ("secao", "Seção",               140),
        ], on_double=lambda e: self._pick())
        tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._tree = tree

        rows = []; i = 0
        for c in cards:
            label = c["label"]
            sec   = c["section"]
            v     = c.get("value")
            vstr  = Formatters.format(v, "decimal", 2) if v is not None else "—"
            iid   = str(i)
            self._payload[iid] = label
            rows.append((iid, (f"🔖  [{label}]", vstr, sec))); i += 1
            for f in c.get("fields", []):
                iid = str(i)
                self._payload[iid] = f"{label}.{f['path']}"
                fv = Formatters.format(f["value"], "decimal", 2)
                rows.append((iid, (f"     ·  [{label}.{f['path']}]", fv, sec))); i += 1

        tree.set_rows(rows)

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 940, 620, parent=parent, min_w=640, min_h=400)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 640, 400, fit_to_content=False)
        enable_fullscreen(self)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())
        self.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._pick())

    def _pick(self):
        sel = self._tree.selection()
        if not sel: return
        self.result = self._payload.get(sel[0])
        self.destroy()


class JsonEditorDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, title, value):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title(title); self.result = None
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); self.transient(parent); self.grab_set()

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(0, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="✓ Aplicar", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._apply).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44); hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text=title, bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"]); body.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                                                            padx=16, pady=14)
        self._editor = JsonGridEditor(body, value=value, height=14)
        self._editor.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 640, 520, parent=parent, min_w=480, min_h=360)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 480, 360, fit_to_content=False)
        enable_fullscreen(self)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())

    def _apply(self):
        self.result = self._editor.get_value(); self.destroy()


class TableColumnsDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, current):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title("Definir colunas"); self.result = None
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); self.transient(parent); self.grab_set()

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(0, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="✓ Aplicar", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._apply).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44); hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text="Colunas da tabela", bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"]); body.pack(fill="both", expand=True,
                                                            padx=16, pady=14)
        tk.Label(body, text="Uma coluna por linha (ordem será preservada):",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(anchor="w")
        self.txt = tk.Text(body, height=10, font=(MONO,10), relief="solid",
                           borderwidth=1, bg="white")
        self.txt.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(4,0))
        self.txt.insert("1.0", "\n".join(current))

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 400, 380, parent=parent, min_w=360, min_h=300)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 360, 300, fit_to_content=False)
        enable_fullscreen(self)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())

    def _apply(self):
        cols = [l.strip() for l in self.txt.get("1.0","end").split("\n") if l.strip()]
        self.result = cols; self.destroy()

# ============================================================
# FORM FIELDS PANEL
# ============================================================
class FormFieldsPanel(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent, fields, initial=None, readonly=False,
                 preview_fn=None, cards_provider=None, bg=None,
                 on_change=None, layout="auto",
                 external_vars=None):
        super().__init__(parent, bg=bg or COLORS["bg"])
        self.fields = fields
        self._field_specs = {f.get("key"): f for f in fields if f.get("key")}
        self.vars = {}
        self.external_vars = external_vars or {}
        self.readonly = readonly
        self.preview_fn = preview_fn
        self.cards_provider = cards_provider
        self._on_change_cb = on_change
        self._raw_debounce = {}
        self._entries = {}
        self.preview_lbl = None
        self._auto_calc_specs = []
        self._auto_values = {}
        self._auto_labels = {}
        self.layout = layout
        self._build(initial or {})

    def _build(self, initial):
        use_grid = (self.layout == "grid") or \
                   any(f.get("group") for f in self.fields)

        if not use_grid:
            self._build_single_column(initial)
        else:
            self._build_grouped(initial)

        self._recompute_auto_calcs()

        if self.preview_fn:
            self.preview_lbl = tk.Label(
                self, text="", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
                font=(FONT, 10, "bold"), anchor="w", justify="left",
                wraplength=760,
            )
            self.preview_lbl.pack(fill="x", pady=(8, 0), padx=4)
            self._update_preview()

    def _build_single_column(self, initial):
        container = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        container.pack(fill="x", expand=False)
        container.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        row = 0
        for f in self.fields:
            key = f["key"];
            ftype = f.get("type", "text")
            val = initial.get(key, f.get("default", ""))
            row = self._build_field(container, row, 0, 1, key, ftype, f, val)
            if ftype == "auto_calc":
                self._auto_calc_specs.append((
                    key, f.get("formula", ""),
                    f.get("unit", "decimal"), f.get("decimals", None),
                ))
            row += 1

    def _build_grouped(self, initial):
        grouped = []
        for f in self.fields:
            g = f.get("group", "")
            if not grouped or grouped[-1][0] != g:
                grouped.append((g, []))
            grouped[-1][1].append(f)

        for gname, gfields in grouped:
            if gname:
                lf = tk.LabelFrame(
                    self, text=f" {gname} ",
                    bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
                    font=(FONT, 9, "bold"), bd=1, relief="solid",
                )
                lf.pack(fill="x", pady=(2, 6), padx=2)
                lf.columnconfigure(0, weight=1, uniform="col")
                lf.columnconfigure(1, weight=1, uniform="col")
                parent = lf
            else:
                parent = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
                parent.pack(fill="x")
                parent.columnconfigure(0, weight=1, uniform="col")
                parent.columnconfigure(1, weight=1, uniform="col")

            row = 0; col = 0
            for f in gfields:
                span = int(f.get("span", 1))
                if span == 2 and col == 1:
                    row += 2; col = 0

                key = f["key"]; ftype = f.get("type", "text")
                val = initial.get(key, f.get("default", ""))
                self._build_field(parent, row, col, span, key, ftype, f, val)

                if ftype == "auto_calc":
                    self._auto_calc_specs.append((
                        key, f.get("formula", ""),
                        f.get("unit", "decimal"), f.get("decimals", None),
                    ))

                if span == 2:
                    row += 2; col = 0
                else:
                    col += 1
                    if col > 1:
                        col = 0; row += 2

    def _build_field(self, parent, row, col, span, key, ftype, f, val):
        ro = self.readonly

        tk.Label(parent, text=f["label"], bg=COLORS["bg"],
                 fg=COLORS["gray_dark"], font=(FONT, 9, "bold"),
                 anchor="w", justify="left", wraplength=320
                 ).grid(row=row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                        sticky="ew", pady=(6, 2), padx=(2, 2))

        entry_row = row + 1

        if ftype == "combo":
            v = tk.StringVar(value=str(val))
            cb = ttk.Combobox(parent, textvariable=v, values=f["values"],
                              state="disabled" if ro else "readonly")
            cb.grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                    sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
            self.vars[key] = v

        elif ftype == "float_calc":
            fr = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
            fr.grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                    sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
            v = tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
            ttk.Entry(fr, textvariable=v,
                      state="disabled" if ro else "normal"
                      ).grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
            if not ro:
                ttk.Button(fr, text="🧮", width=3,
                           command=lambda v=v: YoYCalcDialog(self, v)
                           ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(4, 0))
            v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
            self.vars[key] = v

        elif ftype == "formula_calc":
            fr = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
            fr.grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                    sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
            v = tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
            entry = ttk.Entry(fr, textvariable=v, font=(MONO, 10),
                              state="disabled" if ro else "normal")
            entry.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
            if not ro:
                ttk.Button(fr, text="📋 Inserir CARD", width=16,
                           command=lambda e=entry: self._open_card_picker(e)
                           ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(6, 0))
            v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
            self.vars[key] = v
            self._entries[key] = entry

        elif ftype == "auto_calc":
            fr = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
            fr.grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                    sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
            lbl = tk.Label(
                fr, text="—",
                bg="#f1f4f9", fg=COLORS["navy"],
                font=(FONT, 11, "bold"), anchor="w",
                padx=8, pady=4, relief="solid", borderwidth=1,
                highlightthickness=0,
            )
            lbl.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
            tk.Label(fr, text="🧮 Calculado automaticamente",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                     font=(FONT, 7, "italic"), anchor="w"
                     ).grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(1, 0))
            self.vars[key] = lbl
            self._auto_labels[key] = lbl
            self._auto_values[key] = None

        elif ftype == "tempo_input":
            fr = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
            fr.grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                    sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
            v = tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
            entry = ttk.Entry(fr, textvariable=v, font=(MONO, 10),
                              state="disabled" if ro else "normal")
            entry.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
            tk.Label(fr, text="Ex.: 5,5 (dias)   ou   00:15 (HH:MM)",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                     font=(FONT, 7, "italic"), anchor="w"
                     ).grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", pady=(1, 0))
            v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
            self.vars[key] = v
            self._entries[key] = entry

        elif ftype in ("json", "metrics_calc", "json_grid"):
            self._build_json_field(parent, entry_row, col, span, key, ftype, f, val)

        elif ftype == "text_multi":
            fr = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
            fr.grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                    sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            h = f.get("height", 4)
            txt = tk.Text(fr, height=h, font=(FONT, 10), relief="solid",
                          borderwidth=1, bg="white", wrap="word",
                          state="disabled" if ro else "normal")
            txt.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
            txt.insert("1.0", "" if val is None else str(val))
            if ro:
                txt.config(state="disabled")
            vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(fr, orient="vertical", command=txt.yview)
            txt.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
            vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
            txt.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._update_preview)
            self.vars[key] = txt

        else:
            v = tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
            ttk.Entry(parent, textvariable=v,
                      state="disabled" if ro else "normal"
                      ).grid(row=entry_row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                             sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
            v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
            self.vars[key] = v

        return entry_row

    def _read_field_value(self, key):
        w = self.vars.get(key)
        if w is None: return ""
        if isinstance(w, tk.StringVar):
            return w.get()
        if isinstance(w, tk.Text):
            return w.get("1.0", "end-1c")
        if isinstance(w, dict) and "editor" in w:
            try: return w["editor"].get_value()
            except Exception: return ""
        return ""

    def _wire_auto_sum(self, target_key, sources):
        def _fmt_num(n):
            if n == int(n):
                return str(int(n))
            return str(round(n, 4)).replace(".", ",")

        def _recalc(*_):
            total = 0.0
            algum_valor = False
            for s in sources:
                raw = self._read_field_value(s)
                if raw is None: continue
                raw = str(raw).strip()
                if raw == "": continue
                try:
                    total += float(raw.replace(",", "."))
                    algum_valor = True
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    pass
            if not algum_valor:
                return
            alvo = self.vars.get(target_key)
            if isinstance(alvo, tk.StringVar):
                novo = _fmt_num(total)
                if alvo.get() != novo:
                    alvo.set(novo)

        for s in sources:
            w = self.vars.get(s)
            if isinstance(w, tk.StringVar):
                w.trace_add("write", _recalc)
            elif isinstance(w, tk.Text):
                w.bind("<KeyRelease>", _recalc, add="+")

        _recalc()

    def _build_json_field(self, parent, row, col, span, key, ftype, f, val):
        parsed = self._parse_initial_json(val, ftype)
        fr = tk.Frame(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        fr.grid(row=row, column=col, columnspan=span,
                sticky="ew", pady=(0, 4), padx=(2, 2))
        fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        if self.readonly:
            txt = tk.Text(fr, height=f.get("height", 8), font=(MONO, 9),
                          relief="solid", borderwidth=1, bg="#f8fafc",
                          wrap="none", state="normal")
            txt.insert("1.0", json.dumps(parsed, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
            txt.config(state="disabled")
            txt.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
            vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(fr, orient="vertical", command=txt.yview)
            txt.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
            vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
            self.vars[key] = {"editor": None, "text": txt, "notebook": None,
                              "readonly": True}
            return

        nb = ttk.Notebook(fr)
        nb.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")

        gf = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        nb.add(gf, text="  📊 Grade  ")
        editor = JsonGridEditor(gf, value=parsed, height=f.get("height", 12))
        editor.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=6, pady=6)

        rf = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        nb.add(rf, text="  🔧 JSON  ")
        txt = tk.Text(rf, height=f.get("height", 12), font=(MONO, 9),
                      relief="solid", borderwidth=1, bg="#f8fafc", wrap="none")
        txt.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(rf, orient="vertical", command=txt.yview)
        txt.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        txt.insert("1.0", json.dumps(parsed, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

        self.vars[key] = {"editor": editor, "text": txt, "notebook": nb,
                          "readonly": False}

        def _grid_to_raw(new_val):
            try:
                txt.delete("1.0", "end")
                txt.insert("1.0", json.dumps(new_val, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
            except Exception:
                pass
            self._update_preview()
        editor._on_change = _grid_to_raw

        def _schedule_raw(_e=None):
            prev = self._raw_debounce.get(id(txt))
            if prev:
                try: self.after_cancel(prev)
                except Exception: pass
            self._raw_debounce[id(txt)] = self.after(600, lambda: self._raw_to_grid(key))
        txt.bind("<KeyRelease>", _schedule_raw)
        txt.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda e: self._raw_to_grid(key))

    def _raw_to_grid(self, key):
        w = self.vars.get(key)
        if not isinstance(w, dict) or w.get("readonly"): return
        raw = w["text"].get("1.0", "end").strip()
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(raw) if raw else {}
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            return
        w["editor"].set_value(parsed)
        self._update_preview()

    def _parse_initial_json(self, val, ftype):
        if isinstance(val, (dict, list)): return deepcopy(val)
        if isinstance(val, str):
            s = val.strip()
            if not s: return [] if ftype == "metrics_calc" else {}
            try: return json.loads(s)
            except json.JSONDecodeError: return [] if ftype == "metrics_calc" else {}
        if val is None: return [] if ftype == "metrics_calc" else {}
        return val

    def _read_field_value_raw(self, key):
        if key in self._auto_values:
            return self._auto_values[key]

        w = self.vars.get(key)
        if w is None:
            w = self.external_vars.get(key)
        if w is None:
            return 0.0

        if isinstance(w, tk.StringVar):
            raw = (w.get() or "").strip()
        elif isinstance(w, tk.Text):
            raw = (w.get("1.0", "end-1c") or "").strip()
        elif isinstance(w, dict) and "editor" in w:
            try:
                v = w["editor"].get_value()
                raw = str(v).strip() if v is not None else ""
            except Exception:
                raw = ""
        elif isinstance(w, tk.Label):
            return self._auto_values.get(key, 0.0)
        else:
            return 0.0

        if raw == "":
            return 0.0

        spec = self._field_specs.get(key, {})
