        tempo_unit_key = spec.get("tempo_unidade_key")
        if tempo_unit_key:
            unidade = "dias"
            uw = self.vars.get(tempo_unit_key)
            if uw is None:
                uw = self.external_vars.get(tempo_unit_key)
            if isinstance(uw, tk.StringVar):
                unidade = (uw.get() or "dias")
            parsed = parse_tempo_value(raw, unidade)
            return 0.0 if parsed is None else float(parsed)

        try:
            return float(raw.replace(",", "."))
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return 0.0

    def _recompute_auto_calcs(self):
        if not self._auto_calc_specs:
            return

        for _ in range(2):
            for key, formula, unit, decimals in self._auto_calc_specs:
                ns = {}
                for other_key in self.vars:
                    ns[other_key] = self._read_field_value_raw(other_key)
                ns.pop(key, None)
                result = _safe_auto_eval(formula, ns)
                self._auto_values[key] = result

        self._update_auto_labels()

    def _update_auto_labels(self):
        for key, formula, unit, decimals in self._auto_calc_specs:
            lbl = self._auto_labels.get(key)
            if lbl is None:
                continue
            value = self._auto_values.get(key)
            text = _fmt_auto(value, unit, decimals)
            color = _auto_color(value, unit)
            try:
                lbl.config(text=text, fg=color)
            except tk.TclError:
                pass

    def _update_preview(self, *_):
        self._recompute_auto_calcs()

        if self._on_change_cb:
            try: self._on_change_cb()
            except Exception: logging.exception("on_change do FormFieldsPanel falhou")
        if not self.preview_fn or not self.preview_lbl:
            return
        try:
            result = self.preview_fn(self.get_raw_values())
        except Exception:
            result = None
        if result is None:
            try: self.preview_lbl.config(text="")
            except Exception: pass
            return
        text, color = result if isinstance(result, tuple) else (result, COLORS["navy"])
        try: self.preview_lbl.config(text=text, fg=color)
        except Exception: pass

    def _open_card_picker(self, entry_widget):
        if not self.cards_provider:
            messagebox.showinfo("Sem cards",
                "Nenhum card disponível para referência.", parent=self); return
        try: cards = self.cards_provider()
        except Exception: cards = []
        if not cards:
            messagebox.showinfo("Sem cards",
                "Ainda não há cards cadastrados no painel.", parent=self); return
        dlg = CardPickerDialog(self, cards)
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if not dlg.result: return
        insert_text = f"[{dlg.result}]"
        try:
            pos = entry_widget.index("insert")
            entry_widget.insert(pos, insert_text)
            entry_widget.focus_set()
            entry_widget.icursor(pos + len(insert_text))
        except Exception:
            current = entry_widget.get()
            entry_widget.delete(0, "end")
            entry_widget.insert(0, current + insert_text)

    def get_raw_values(self):
        raw = {}
        for f in self.fields:
            key = f["key"]; ftype = f.get("type", "text")
            w = self.vars[key]
            if ftype in ("json", "metrics_calc", "json_grid"):
                if isinstance(w, dict) and w.get("readonly"):
                    txt = w["text"].get("1.0", "end").strip()
                    raw[key] = txt
                else:
                    raw[key] = json.dumps(w["editor"].get_value(), ensure_ascii=False)
            elif ftype == "text_multi":
                raw[key] = w.get("1.0", "end-1c")
            else:
                raw[key] = w.get()
        return raw

    def get_values(self):
        result = {}
        for f in self.fields:
            key = f["key"]; ftype = f.get("type", "text")
            w = self.vars[key]

            if ftype == "auto_calc":
                v = self._auto_values.get(key)
                if v is None:
                    result[key] = ""
                elif float(v).is_integer():
                    result[key] = int(v)
                else:
                    result[key] = v
                continue

            if ftype in ("json", "metrics_calc", "json_grid"):
                if isinstance(w, dict) and w.get("readonly"):
                    txt = w["text"].get("1.0", "end").strip()
                    try: result[key] = json.loads(txt) if txt else {}
                    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                        raise ValueError(f"Campo '{f['label']}': {e}")
                else:
                    result[key] = w["editor"].get_value()
            elif ftype == "text_multi":
                result[key] = w.get("1.0", "end").rstrip("\n")
            elif ftype == "int":
                v = str(w.get()).strip()
                result[key] = int(v) if v else 0
            elif ftype in ("float", "float_calc"):
                v = str(w.get()).strip().replace(",", ".")
                result[key] = float(v) if v else 0.0
            else:
                result[key] = str(w.get()).strip()
        return result

# ============================================================
# CONSOLIDATION RULE PANEL
# ============================================================
class ConsolidationRulePanel(tk.Frame):
    REGRAS = [
        ("soma",    "Somar BR + PY"),
        ("br_only", "Usar apenas BR"),
        ("py_only", "Usar apenas PY"),
        ("formula", "Fórmula customizada"),
    ]
    _LABEL_TO_KEY = {lbl: k for k, lbl in REGRAS}
    _KEY_TO_LABEL = {k: lbl for k, lbl in REGRAS}

    def __init__(self, parent, initial_pais="ambos",
                 initial_cons=None, cards_provider=None):
        super().__init__(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        self.cards_provider = cards_provider
        cons = _as_dict(initial_cons) or {"tipo": "soma"}

        tk.Label(self, text="Este card aparece em:",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT, 9, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 2))
        self._pais_var = tk.StringVar(value=initial_pais or "ambos")
        pais_frame = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        pais_frame.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 12))
        for v in ("BR", "PY", "ambos"):
            tk.Radiobutton(
                pais_frame, text=v, value=v, variable=self._pais_var,
                bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"], font=(FONT, 9),
                activebackground=COLORS["bg"], selectcolor="white",
                highlightthickness=0, borderwidth=0,
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 14))

        tk.Label(self, text="Regra de consolidação:",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT, 9, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 2))
        self._tipo_var = tk.StringVar(value=cons.get("tipo", "soma"))
        self._combo = ttk.Combobox(
            self, values=[lbl for _, lbl in self.REGRAS], state="readonly",
        )
        self._combo.set(self._KEY_TO_LABEL.get(self._tipo_var.get(), "Somar BR + PY"))
        self._combo.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 6))
        self._combo.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", self._on_tipo_change)

        self._formula_frame = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        tk.Label(self._formula_frame, text="Fórmula:",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT, 9, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 2))
        fr = tk.Frame(self._formula_frame, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        fr.pack(fill="x")
        fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._formula_var = tk.StringVar(value=cons.get("formula", ""))
        entry = ttk.Entry(fr, textvariable=self._formula_var, font=(MONO, 10))
        entry.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
        ttk.Button(fr, text="📋 Inserir CARD", width=16,
                   command=lambda e=entry: self._open_card_picker(e)
                   ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(6, 0))
        self._formula_entry = entry

        tk.Label(self._formula_frame,
                 text="Use [Card] para o consolidado (BR+PY), [Card.BR] ou [Card.PY] para um país.",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                 font=(FONT, 8, "italic"), anchor="w",
                 justify="left", wraplength=560).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 0))

        if self._tipo_var.get() == "formula":
            self._formula_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 6))

    def _on_tipo_change(self, _e=None):
        lbl = self._combo.get()
        key = self._LABEL_TO_KEY.get(lbl, "soma")
        self._tipo_var.set(key)
        if key == "formula":
            self._formula_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 6))
        else:
            self._formula_frame.pack_forget()

    def _open_card_picker(self, entry_widget):
        if not self.cards_provider:
            messagebox.showinfo("Sem cards",
                "Nenhum card disponível para referência.", parent=self); return
        try: cards = self.cards_provider()
        except Exception: cards = []
        if not cards:
            messagebox.showinfo("Sem cards",
                "Ainda não há cards cadastrados no painel.", parent=self); return
        dlg = CardPickerDialog(self, cards)
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if not dlg.result: return
        insert_text = f"[{dlg.result}]"
        try:
            pos = entry_widget.index("insert")
            entry_widget.insert(pos, insert_text)
            entry_widget.focus_set()
            entry_widget.icursor(pos + len(insert_text))
        except Exception:
            current = entry_widget.get()
            entry_widget.delete(0, "end")
            entry_widget.insert(0, current + insert_text)

    def get_pais(self):
        return self._pais_var.get()

    def get_consolidacao(self):
        tipo = self._tipo_var.get()
        cons = {"tipo": tipo}
        if tipo == "formula":
            cons["formula"] = self._formula_var.get().strip()
        return cons

# ============================================================
# MULTI COUNTRY FORM DIALOG
# ============================================================
def _partition_fields(fields):
    top, multi = [], []
    for f in fields:
        k = f.get("key", "")
        if k in _MULTI_FIELDS:
            multi.append(f)
        else:
            top.append(f)
    return top, multi


class MultiCountryFormDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, title, fields, initial=None,
                 width=760, preview_fn=None, cards_provider=None,
                 default_pais="ambos"):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title(title)
        self.result = None
        self.fields = fields
        self.preview_fn = preview_fn
        self.cards_provider = cards_provider

        self._initial = deepcopy(initial) if initial else {}
        if self._initial and not _item_has_multicountry(self._initial):
            _upgrade_item_to_multicountry(self._initial)

        valores = _as_dict(self._initial.get("valores"))
        self._init_valores = {
            "BR":    deepcopy(_as_dict(valores.get("BR"))),
            "PY":    deepcopy(_as_dict(valores.get("PY"))),
            "BR+PY": deepcopy(_as_dict(valores.get(CONSOLIDATED_VIEW))),
        }
        self._init_pais = self._initial.get("pais") or default_pais or "ambos"
        self._init_cons = self._initial.get("consolidacao") or {"tipo": "soma"}

        self._top_fields, self._multi_fields = _partition_fields(fields)

        self._build_ui(width)

    def _build_ui(self, width):
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"])
        self.transient(self.master)
        self.grab_set()

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(0, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(
            side="right", padx=(8, 0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="💾 Salvar", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self.on_save).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44)
        hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text=self.title(), bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT, 12, "bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = make_scrollable_body(self, bg=COLORS["bg"], padding=(16, 12))

        if self._top_fields:
            top_lf = tk.LabelFrame(body, text=" Comum a todos os países ",
                                   bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
                                   font=(FONT, 9, "bold"), bd=1, relief="solid")
            top_lf.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))

            tem_format = any(f.get("key") == "format" for f in self._top_fields)
            self._top_panel = FormFieldsPanel(
                top_lf, self._top_fields,
                initial=self._initial,
                bg=COLORS["bg"],
                preview_fn=preview_format_rule if tem_format else None,
            )
            self._top_panel.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=8)
        else:
            self._top_panel = None

        def _wrap_preview(base_preview):
            if base_preview is None:
                return None

            def wrapped(raw):
                try:
                    top = self._top_panel.get_values() if self._top_panel else {}
                except Exception:
                    top = {}
                merged = {**top, **raw}
                return base_preview(merged)

            return wrapped

        self._preview_wrapped = _wrap_preview(self.preview_fn)

        nb = ttk.Notebook(body)
        nb.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._nb = nb

        br_frame = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        nb.add(br_frame, text="  BR  ")
        _external = self._top_panel.vars if self._top_panel else {}

        self._br_panel = FormFieldsPanel(
            br_frame, self._multi_fields,
            initial=self._init_valores["BR"],
            preview_fn=self._preview_wrapped,
            cards_provider=self.cards_provider,
            bg=COLORS["bg"],
            external_vars=_external,
        )
        self._br_panel.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=8, pady=8)

        py_frame = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        nb.add(py_frame, text="  PY  ")
        self._py_panel = FormFieldsPanel(
            py_frame, self._multi_fields,
            initial=self._init_valores["PY"],
            preview_fn=self._preview_wrapped,
            cards_provider=self.cards_provider,
            bg=COLORS["bg"],
            external_vars=_external,
        )
        self._py_panel.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=8, pady=8)

        cons_frame = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        nb.add(cons_frame, text="  BR+PY  ")

        rule_lf = tk.LabelFrame(cons_frame, text=" Regra ",
                                bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
                                font=(FONT, 9, "bold"), bd=1, relief="solid")
        rule_lf.pack(fill="x", pady=(4, 8))
        self._rule_panel = ConsolidationRulePanel(
            rule_lf,
            initial_pais=self._init_pais,
            initial_cons=self._init_cons,
            cards_provider=self.cards_provider,
        )
        self._rule_panel.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=8)

        preview_lf = tk.LabelFrame(
            cons_frame,
            text=" Resultado consolidado (somente leitura) ",
            bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
            font=(FONT, 9, "bold"), bd=1, relief="solid",
        )
        preview_lf.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

        tk.Label(preview_lf,
                 text="Recalculado automaticamente ao publicar. "
                      "Valores abaixo são os da última publicação.",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                 font=(FONT, 8, "italic"), anchor="w",
                 justify="left", wraplength=640
                 ).pack(anchor="w", padx=10, pady=(4, 2))

        self._cons_panel = FormFieldsPanel(
            preview_lf, self._multi_fields,
            initial=self._init_valores[CONSOLIDATED_VIEW],
            readonly=True,
            bg=COLORS["bg"],
        )
        self._cons_panel.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10, pady=8)

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 1100, 720, parent=self.master,
                             min_w=720, min_h=520)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 720, 520, fit_to_content=False)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())
        enable_fullscreen(self)

    def on_save(self):
        result = {}

        if self._top_panel is not None:
            try:
                result.update(self._top_panel.get_values())
            except ValueError as e:
                messagebox.showerror("Erro", str(e), parent=self); return
            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                messagebox.showerror("JSON inválido", str(e), parent=self); return

        try:
            br_values = self._br_panel.get_values()
        except ValueError as e:
            messagebox.showerror("Erro em BR", str(e), parent=self); return
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            messagebox.showerror("JSON inválido em BR", str(e), parent=self); return

        try:
            py_values = self._py_panel.get_values()
        except ValueError as e:
            messagebox.showerror("Erro em PY", str(e), parent=self); return
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            messagebox.showerror("JSON inválido em PY", str(e), parent=self); return

        for k, v in self._initial.items():
            if k in result or k in ("valores", "pais", "consolidacao"):
                continue
            result[k] = v

        result["pais"] = self._rule_panel.get_pais()
        result["consolidacao"] = self._rule_panel.get_consolidacao()

        valores_out = {
            "BR": br_values or {},
            "PY": py_values or {},
        }
        if self._init_valores[CONSOLIDATED_VIEW]:
            valores_out[CONSOLIDATED_VIEW] = self._init_valores[CONSOLIDATED_VIEW]
        result["valores"] = valores_out

        self.result = result
        self.destroy()

# ============================================================
# FORM DIALOG (legado, single-country)
# ============================================================
class FormDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, title, fields, initial=None, width=700,
                 preview_fn=None, cards_provider=None):
        super().__init__(parent); self.title(title); self.result=None
        self.fields=fields; self.vars={}; self.preview_fn=preview_fn
        self.cards_provider=cards_provider
        self._raw_debounce = {}
        self._auto_values = {}
        self._auto_calc_specs = []
        self._auto_labels = {}
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); self.transient(parent); self.grab_set()

        btns=tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(0, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="💾 Salvar (Enter)", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self.on_save).pack(side="right")

        hdr=tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44); hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text=title, bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = make_scrollable_body(self, bg=COLORS["bg"], padding=(16, 14))
        initial=initial or {}; row=0
        for f in fields:
            tk.Label(body, text=f["label"], bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                     font=(FONT,9,"bold"), anchor="w", justify="left",
                     wraplength=600).grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(6,2))
            row+=1
            key=f["key"]; ftype=f.get("type","text")
            val=initial.get(key, f.get("default",""))
            if ftype=="combo":
                v=tk.StringVar(value=str(val))
                ttk.Combobox(body, textvariable=v, values=f["values"],
                             state="readonly").grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(0,4))
                v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
                self.vars[key]=v
            elif ftype=="float_calc":
                fr=tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"]); fr.grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(0,4))
                fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
                v=tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
                ttk.Entry(fr, textvariable=v).grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
                ttk.Button(fr, text="🧮", width=3,
                           command=lambda v=v: YoYCalcDialog(self, v)).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(4,0))
                v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
                self.vars[key]=v
            elif ftype == "formula_calc":
                fr = tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"])
                fr.grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(0,4))
                fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
                v = tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
                entry = ttk.Entry(fr, textvariable=v, font=(MONO,10))
                entry.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
                ttk.Button(fr, text="📋 Inserir CARD", width=16,
                           command=lambda e=entry: self._open_card_picker(e)
                           ).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(6,0))
                v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
                self.vars[key] = v
                if not hasattr(self, "_entries"): self._entries = {}
                self._entries[key] = entry
            elif ftype in ("json","metrics_calc","json_grid"):
                self._build_json_field(body, row, key, ftype, f, val)
                if ftype=="metrics_calc":
                    row += 1
                    ttk.Button(body, text="🧮 Calcular status dos indicadores",
                               command=lambda k=key: self._calc_metrics_from_editor(k)
                               ).grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="w",pady=(0,4))
            elif ftype=="text_multi":
                fr=tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"]); fr.grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(0,4))
                h=f.get("height", 4)
                txt=tk.Text(fr, height=h, font=(FONT,10), relief="solid",
                            borderwidth=1, bg="white", wrap="word")
                txt.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
                txt.insert("1.0", "" if val is None else str(val))
                vsb=ttk.Scrollbar(fr, orient="vertical", command=txt.yview)
                txt.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set); vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
                txt.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._update_preview)
                self.vars[key]=txt
            else:
                v=tk.StringVar(value="" if val is None else str(val))
                ttk.Entry(body, textvariable=v).grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(0,4))
                v.trace_add("write", self._update_preview)
                self.vars[key]=v
            row+=1
        body.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        if self.preview_fn:
            self.preview_lbl=tk.Label(body, text="", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["navy"],
                                       font=(FONT,10,"bold"), anchor="w", justify="left",
                                       wraplength=620)
            self.preview_lbl.grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(6,0))
            row+=1

        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())
        self.bind("<Control-Return>", lambda e: self.on_save())

        fit_window_to_screen(self, width, 580, parent=parent,
                             min_w=520, min_h=400)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 520, 400, fit_to_content=False)
        self._update_preview()
        enable_fullscreen(self)

    def _build_json_field(self, body, row, key, ftype, f, val):
        parsed = self._parse_initial_json(val, ftype)
        fr=tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"]); fr.grid(row=row,column=0,sticky="ew",pady=(0,4))
        fr.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        nb = ttk.Notebook(fr); nb.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")

        gf = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"]); nb.add(gf, text="  📊 Grade  ")
        editor = JsonGridEditor(gf, value=parsed, height=f.get("height", 12))
        editor.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=6, pady=6)

        rf = tk.Frame(nb, bg=COLORS["bg"]); nb.add(rf, text="  🔧 JSON  ")
        txt=tk.Text(rf, height=f.get("height", 12), font=(MONO,9),
                    relief="solid", borderwidth=1, bg="#f8fafc", wrap="none")
        txt.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
        vsb=ttk.Scrollbar(rf, orient="vertical", command=txt.yview)
        txt.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set); vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y")
        txt.insert("1.0", json.dumps(parsed, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

        self.vars[key] = {"editor": editor, "text": txt, "notebook": nb}

        def _grid_to_raw(new_val):
            try:
                txt.delete("1.0","end")
                txt.insert("1.0", json.dumps(new_val, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
            except Exception: pass
            self._update_preview()
        editor._on_change = _grid_to_raw

        def _schedule_raw(_e=None):
            prev = self._raw_debounce.get(id(txt))
            if prev:
                try: self.after_cancel(prev)
                except Exception: pass
            self._raw_debounce[id(txt)] = self.after(600, lambda: self._raw_to_grid(key))
        txt.bind("<KeyRelease>", _schedule_raw)
        txt.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda e: self._raw_to_grid(key))

    def _raw_to_grid(self, key):
        w = self.vars.get(key)
        if not isinstance(w, dict): return
        raw = w["text"].get("1.0","end").strip()
        try: parsed = json.loads(raw) if raw else {}
        except json.JSONDecodeError: return
        w["editor"].set_value(parsed)
        self._update_preview()

    def _parse_initial_json(self, val, ftype):
        if isinstance(val, (dict, list)): return deepcopy(val)
        if isinstance(val, str):
            s = val.strip()
            if not s: return [] if ftype == "metrics_calc" else {}
            try: return json.loads(s)
            except json.JSONDecodeError: return [] if ftype == "metrics_calc" else {}
        if val is None: return [] if ftype == "metrics_calc" else {}
        return val

    def _get_raw_values(self):
        raw={}
        for f in self.fields:
            key=f["key"]; ftype=f.get("type","text")
            w=self.vars[key]

            if ftype == "auto_calc":
                raw[key] = self._auto_values.get(key, "")
                continue

            if ftype in ("json","metrics_calc","json_grid"):
                raw[key] = json.dumps(w["editor"].get_value(), ensure_ascii=False)
            elif ftype == "text_multi":
                raw[key] = w.get("1.0","end-1c")
            else:
                raw[key] = w.get()
        return raw

    def _update_preview(self, *_):
        if not self.preview_fn: return
        try: result=self.preview_fn(self._get_raw_values())
        except Exception: result=None
        if result is None:
            try: self.preview_lbl.config(text="")
            except Exception: pass
            return
        text, color = result if isinstance(result, tuple) else (result, COLORS["navy"])
        try: self.preview_lbl.config(text=text, fg=color)
        except Exception: pass

    def _open_card_picker(self, entry_widget):
        if not self.cards_provider:
            messagebox.showinfo("Sem cards",
                "Nenhum card disponível para referência.", parent=self); return
        try: cards = self.cards_provider()
        except Exception: cards = []
        if not cards:
            messagebox.showinfo("Sem cards",
                "Ainda não há cards cadastrados no painel.", parent=self); return
        dlg = CardPickerDialog(self, cards)
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if not dlg.result: return
        insert_text = f"[{dlg.result}]"
        try:
            pos = entry_widget.index("insert")
            entry_widget.insert(pos, insert_text)
            entry_widget.focus_set()
            entry_widget.icursor(pos + len(insert_text))
        except Exception:
            current = entry_widget.get()
            entry_widget.delete(0, "end")
            entry_widget.insert(0, current + insert_text)

    def _calc_metrics_from_editor(self, key):
        arr = self.vars[key]["editor"].get_value()
        if not isinstance(arr, list):
            messagebox.showinfo("Status calculado", "O valor não é uma lista.", parent=self); return
        linhas=[]
        icone={"green":"🟢","yellow":"🟡","red":"🔴","gray":"⚪"}
        for idx,m in enumerate(arr, start=1):
            if not isinstance(m, dict):
                linhas.append(f"{idx}. (item inválido)"); continue
            tier=Calc.status_tier(m.get("valor"), m.get("meta"), m.get("direction"))
            p=Calc.percentual(m.get("valor"), m.get("meta"))
            pct=f"{Calc.format_br(p)}%" if p is not None else "—"
            rotulo=m.get("titulo") or m.get("label") or f"item {idx}"
            linhas.append(f"{icone.get(tier,'⚪')} {rotulo}: {pct}")
        messagebox.showinfo("Status calculado (aproximação de 3 faixas)",
                            "\n".join(linhas) if linhas else "Nenhum item no array.",
                            parent=self)

    def on_save(self):
        result={}
        for f in self.fields:
            key=f["key"]; ftype=f.get("type","text")
            try:
                if ftype in ("json","metrics_calc","json_grid"):
                    result[key] = self.vars[key]["editor"].get_value()
                elif ftype=="text_multi":
                    result[key]=self.vars[key].get("1.0","end").rstrip("\n")
                elif ftype=="int":
                    v=str(self.vars[key].get()).strip()
                    result[key]=int(v) if v else 0
                elif ftype in ("float","float_calc"):
                    v=str(self.vars[key].get()).strip().replace(",",".")
                    result[key]=float(v) if v else 0.0
                else:
                    result[key]=str(self.vars[key].get()).strip()
            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
                messagebox.showerror("JSON inválido", f"Campo '{f['label']}':\n{e}", parent=self); return
            except Exception as e:
                messagebox.showerror("Erro", f"Campo '{f['label']}':\n{e}", parent=self); return
        self.result=result; self.destroy()

# ============================================================
# PRÉVIAS DE CÁLCULO
# ============================================================
def preview_status_meta(raw):
    d_str = str(raw.get("dentro") or "").strip()
    f_str = str(raw.get("fora")   or "").strip()
    if d_str or f_str:
        d = Calc.parse_number(d_str) if d_str else 0
        f = Calc.parse_number(f_str) if f_str else 0
        total = d + f
        if total <= 0:
            return ("Dentro + Fora = 0 — não é possível calcular o indicador",
                    COLORS["text_gray"])
        pct = (d / total) * 100
        meta_num = Calc.parse_number(raw.get("meta"))
        if meta_num > 0:
            tier = Calc.status_tier(pct, meta_num, raw.get("direction"))
            return (
                f"📊 Indicador: {Calc.format_br(pct)}%   "
                f"(Dentro {d} + Fora {f} = Total {total})   "
                f"—  Meta: {raw.get('meta')}  →  {TIER_LABEL[tier]}",
                COLORS[TIER_COLOR_KEY[tier]],
            )
        return (
            f"📊 Indicador: {Calc.format_br(pct)}%   "
            f"(Dentro {d} + Fora {f} = Total {total})   —   meta não definida",
            COLORS["text_gray"],
        )

    meta = (raw.get("meta") or "").strip()
    if not meta or meta == "-":
        return ("Sem meta definida — status não pode ser calculado",
                COLORS["text_gray"])
    p = Calc.percentual(raw.get("valor"), meta)
    if p is None:
        return ("Meta inválida (zero ou não numérica)", COLORS["gray_dark"])
    tier = Calc.status_tier(raw.get("valor"), meta, raw.get("direction"))
    return (f"📊 {Calc.format_br(p)}% da meta — {TIER_LABEL[tier]}",
            COLORS[TIER_COLOR_KEY[tier]])

def preview_tempo_medio(raw):
    unidade = raw.get("unidade_tempo")
    if not unidade:
        meta_raw = str(raw.get("meta", "")).strip()
        unidade = "hhmm" if ":" in meta_raw else "dias"

    meta_v  = parse_tempo_value(raw.get("meta"),     unidade)
    tempo_v = parse_tempo_value(raw.get("tempoMes"), unidade)

    if tempo_v is None:
        return ("⏱ Informe o tempo médio do mês para ver o status.",
                COLORS["text_gray"])
    if meta_v is None or meta_v <= 0:
        return ("⚠ Meta inválida — deve ser maior que zero.",
                COLORS["red"])

    tier = calc_tempo_status(tempo_v, meta_v, raw.get("direction"))
    diff = tempo_v - meta_v
    diff_txt = format_tempo_diff(diff, unidade)

    unidade_lbl = " dias" if (unidade == "dias" and diff != 0) else ""
    return (
        f"⏱ Tempo médio: {format_tempo_value(tempo_v, unidade)}   "
        f"Meta: {format_tempo_value(meta_v, unidade)}   "
        f"Diferença: {diff_txt}{unidade_lbl}   →   "
        f"{TIER_LABEL[tier]}",
        COLORS[TIER_COLOR_KEY[tier]],
    )


def preview_kpi_percentual(raw):
    meta=(raw.get("meta") or "").strip()
    if not meta or meta=="-": return None
    p=Calc.percentual(raw.get("valor"), meta)
    if p is None: return ("Meta inválida (zero ou não numérica)", COLORS["gray_dark"])
    return (f"📊 {Calc.format_br(p)}% da meta informada", COLORS["navy"])


def preview_vol_yoy(raw):
    delta=Calc.variacao(raw.get("ano2026"), raw.get("ano2025"))
    if delta is None:
        return ("Informe os dois valores para calcular a variação YoY", COLORS["text_gray"])
    texto,_=Calc.format_variacao(delta)
    return (f"Variação YoY (2026 vs 2025): {texto}", Calc.format_variacao(delta)[1])


def preview_vol_budget(raw):
    try:
        a26=json.loads(raw.get("ano2026") or "{}")
        a25=json.loads(raw.get("ano2025") or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return ("JSON inválido em 2026/2025 — corrija para calcular o desvio", COLORS["red"])
    if not isinstance(a26, dict) or not isinstance(a25, dict): return None
    partes=[]
    dm=Calc.variacao(a26.get("mes"), a25.get("mes"))
    if dm is not None:
        t,_=Calc.format_variacao(dm); partes.append(f"Mês: {t}")
    dy=Calc.variacao(a26.get("ytd"), a25.get("ytd"))
    if dy is not None:
        t,_=Calc.format_variacao(dy); partes.append(f"YTD: {t}")
    if not partes:
        return ("Sem dados suficientes para calcular desvio (2025 vazio ou zero)", COLORS["text_gray"])
    return ("Desvio 2026 vs 2025 — " + "    ".join(partes), COLORS["navy"])


def preview_format_rule(raw):
    fmt = (raw.get("format") or "auto").strip()
    if fmt == "auto":
        return ("Modo automático — valor exibido como digitado (strings) "
                "ou em formato BR (números).", COLORS["text_gray"])
    try:
        dec = int(raw.get("decimals") or 2)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        dec = 2
    exemplos = [
        Formatters.format(1234567.89, fmt, dec),
        Formatters.format(0.845, fmt, dec),
        Formatters.format(42, fmt, dec),
    ]
    return ("Exemplos:  " + "    •    ".join(exemplos), COLORS["navy"])

# EDITORES DE PAINÉIS DE DESTAQUE (UI visual)

class ChartEditorDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, title, initial=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title(title); self.result = None
        self._initial = deepcopy(initial) or {}
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); self.transient(parent); self.grab_set()

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(0, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="💾 Salvar", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._save).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44); hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text=title, bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = make_scrollable_body(self, bg=COLORS["bg"], padding=(16, 14))
        self._vars = {}

        def _lbl(t): tk.Label(body, text=t, bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                              font=(FONT,9,"bold"), anchor="w").pack(anchor="w", pady=(6,2))

        _lbl("Título do gráfico")
        v_title = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get("title",""))
        ttk.Entry(body, textvariable=v_title).pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
        self._vars["title"] = v_title

        duo = tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"]); duo.pack(fill="x", pady=(4,0))
        duo.columnconfigure(0, weight=1); duo.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

        tk.Label(duo, text="Tipo", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", padx=(0,6))
        tk.Label(duo, text="Cor principal", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="w", padx=(6,0))
        v_type = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get("type","vertical"))
        ttk.Combobox(duo, textvariable=v_type,
                     values=["vertical","horizontal","line","vertical-dual", "vertical-stacked"],
                     state="readonly").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=(0,6))
        v_color = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get("color","var(--navy)"))
        ttk.Combobox(duo, textvariable=v_color,
                     values=["var(--navy)","var(--red)","var(--gold-deep)","var(--yellow)",""],
                     state="readonly").grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(6,0))
        self._vars["type"] = v_type
        self._vars["color"] = v_color

        self._dual_frame = tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"])

        duo2 = tk.Frame(self._dual_frame, bg=COLORS["bg"]); duo2.pack(fill="x", pady=(6,0))
        duo2.columnconfigure(0, weight=1); duo2.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        tk.Label(duo2, text="Rótulo 1ª série", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", padx=(0,6))
        tk.Label(duo2, text="Rótulo 2ª série", bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="w", padx=(6,0))
        v_label1 = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get("label1","Total"))
        v_label2 = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get("label2","Em Aberto"))
        ttk.Entry(duo2, textvariable=v_label1).grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew", padx=(0,6))
        ttk.Entry(duo2, textvariable=v_label2).grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(6,0))
        self._vars["label1"] = v_label1
        self._vars["label2"] = v_label2

        tk.Label(self._dual_frame, text="Cor secundária (2ª série)",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(6,2))
        v_color2 = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get("color2","var(--red)"))
        ttk.Combobox(self._dual_frame, textvariable=v_color2,
                     values=["var(--navy)","var(--red)","var(--gold-deep)","var(--yellow)",""],
                     state="readonly").pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
        self._vars["color2"] = v_color2

        v_ispercent = tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(self._initial.get("isPercent", False)))
        v_sort      = tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(self._initial.get("sort", False)))
        ttk.Checkbutton(self._dual_frame, text="Valores já em %",
                        variable=v_ispercent).pack(anchor="w")
        ttk.Checkbutton(self._dual_frame, text="Ordenar por total",
                        variable=v_sort).pack(anchor="w")
        self._vars["isPercent"] = v_ispercent
        self._vars["sort"] = v_sort

        tk.Label(body, text="Dados (séries)",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(10,2))
        tk.Label(body,
                 text="Cada série é um objeto com 'label' e 'valor' "
                      "(para tipo 'vertical-dual' use 'valorTotal' e 'valorAberto').",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                 font=(FONT,8,"italic")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,4))
        self._data_editor = JsonGridEditor(body,
                                            value=self._initial.get("data", []),
                                            height=14,
                                            highlight_empty=True)
        self._data_editor.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(0,4))
        enable_fullscreen(self)

        def _on_type_change(*_):
            if v_type.get() == "vertical-dual":
                self._dual_frame.pack(fill="x", after=duo)
            else:
                self._dual_frame.pack_forget()
        v_type.trace_add("write", _on_type_change)
        _on_type_change()

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 700, 700, parent=parent, min_w=560, min_h=500)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 560, 500, fit_to_content=False)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())

    def _save(self):
        data = self._data_editor.get_value()
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            messagebox.showerror("Erro", "Dados devem ser uma lista de séries.", parent=self); return

        def _serie_tem_conteudo(s):
            if not isinstance(s, dict): return False
            for v in s.values():
                if v is None: continue
                if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() == "": continue
                return True
            return False

        data_limpa = [s for s in data if _serie_tem_conteudo(s)]
        removidas = len(data) - len(data_limpa)

        result = {
            "title": self._vars["title"].get().strip(),
            "type":  self._vars["type"].get().strip(),
            "color": self._vars["color"].get().strip(),
            "data":  data_limpa,
        }
        if result["type"] == "vertical-dual":
            result["color2"]   = self._vars["color2"].get().strip()
            result["label1"]   = self._vars["label1"].get().strip()
            result["label2"]   = self._vars["label2"].get().strip()
            result["isPercent"] = bool(self._vars["isPercent"].get())
            result["sort"]      = bool(self._vars["sort"].get())
        for k, v in self._initial.items():
            if k not in result:
                result[k] = v

        if removidas > 0:
            try:
                Toast(self.master,
                      f"{removidas} série(s) vazia(s) removida(s) automaticamente.",
                      "info", 3000)
            except Exception:
                pass

        self.result = result; self.destroy()


class ChartsListEditor(tk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent, value=None, kind="exportacao"):
        super().__init__(parent, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        self._charts = deepcopy(_as_list(value))
        self._kind = kind
        limite = 3 if kind == "sac" else 6

        bar = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"]); bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="➕ Adicionar", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._add).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="✏ Editar", command=self._edit).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="📋 Duplicar", command=self._dup).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="↑ Subir", command=self._move_up).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="↓ Descer", command=self._move_down).pack(side="left", padx=(0,4))
        ttk.Button(bar, text="🗑 Remover", style="Danger.TButton",
                   command=self._del).pack(side="left")
        tk.Label(bar, text=f"(limite: {limite} gráficos)",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                 font=(FONT,8,"italic")).pack(side="right")

        cont = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                        highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
        cont.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self._tree = ttk.Treeview(cont, columns=("pos","title","type","color","series"),
                                   show="headings", selectmode="browse", height=6)
        self._tree.heading("pos",    text="#")
        self._tree.heading("title",  text="Título")
        self._tree.heading("type",   text="Tipo")
        self._tree.heading("color",  text="Cor")
        self._tree.heading("series", text="Séries")
        self._tree.column("pos",     width=30,  anchor="center", stretch=False)
        self._tree.column("title",   width=260, anchor="w", stretch=True)
        self._tree.column("type",    width=110, anchor="w", stretch=False)
        self._tree.column("color",   width=150, anchor="w", stretch=False)
        self._tree.column("series",  width=70,  anchor="center", stretch=False)
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="vertical", command=self._tree.yview)
        hsb = ttk.Scrollbar(cont, orient="horizontal", command=self._tree.xview)
        self._tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set, xscrollcommand=hsb.set)
        self._tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vsb.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hsb.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        cont.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1); cont.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self._tree.bind("<Double-1>", lambda e: self._edit())
        self._refresh()

    def get_value(self): return deepcopy(self._charts)

    def _limit(self): return 3 if self._kind == "sac" else 6

    def _refresh(self):
        self._tree.delete(*self._tree.get_children())
        for i, c in enumerate(self._charts):
            if not isinstance(c, dict): continue
            self._tree.insert("", "end", iid=str(i), values=(
                i+1, c.get("title",""), c.get("type",""),
                c.get("color",""), len(_as_list(c.get("data")))))

    def _sel_idx(self):
        s = self._tree.selection()
        if not s: return None
        try: return int(s[0])
        except (ValueError, IndexError): return None

    def _add(self):
        if len(self._charts) >= self._limit():
            messagebox.showwarning("Limite",
                f"Máximo {self._limit()} gráficos neste painel.", parent=self); return
        dlg = ChartEditorDialog(self, "Novo Gráfico")
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result:
            self._charts.append(dlg.result); self._refresh()

    def _edit(self):
        i = self._sel_idx()
        if i is None: return
        dlg = ChartEditorDialog(self, "Editar Gráfico", initial=self._charts[i])
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result:
            self._charts[i] = dlg.result; self._refresh()

    def _dup(self):
        i = self._sel_idx()
        if i is None: return
        if len(self._charts) >= self._limit():
            messagebox.showwarning("Limite",
                f"Máximo {self._limit()} gráficos.", parent=self); return
        c = deepcopy(self._charts[i])
        c["title"] = (c.get("title","") + " (cópia)").strip()
        self._charts.insert(i+1, c); self._refresh()

    def _del(self):
        i = self._sel_idx()
        if i is None: return
        if not messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar", "Remover este gráfico?", parent=self): return
        del self._charts[i]; self._refresh()

    def _move_up(self):
        i = self._sel_idx()
        if i is None or i == 0: return
        self._charts[i-1], self._charts[i] = self._charts[i], self._charts[i-1]
        self._refresh(); self._tree.selection_set(str(i-1))

    def _move_down(self):
        i = self._sel_idx()
        if i is None or i >= len(self._charts)-1: return
        self._charts[i+1], self._charts[i] = self._charts[i], self._charts[i+1]
        self._refresh(); self._tree.selection_set(str(i+1))


class PainelEditorDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, title, initial=None, kind="exportacao"):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.title(title); self.result = None
        self._initial = deepcopy(initial) or {}
        self._kind = kind
        self.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); self.transient(parent); self.grab_set()

        btns = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(self, btns, padx=16, pady=(10, 14))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=self.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        ttk.Button(btns, text="💾 Salvar", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._save).pack(side="right")

        hdr = tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44); hdr.pack(fill="x")
        hdr.pack_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(hdr, text=title, bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=18)

        body = make_scrollable_body(self, bg=COLORS["bg"], padding=(16, 14))

        self._vars = {}
        grid = tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"]); grid.pack(fill="x")
        grid.columnconfigure(0, weight=1); grid.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

        def add_entry(r, c, label, key, default=""):
            tk.Label(grid, text=label, bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                     font=(FONT,9,"bold")).grid(row=r*2, column=c, sticky="w",
                                                 padx=(0,8), pady=(6,2))
            v = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get(key, default))
            ttk.Entry(grid, textvariable=v).grid(row=r*2+1, column=c,
                                                  sticky="ew", padx=(0,8), pady=(0,4))
            self._vars[key] = v

        def add_combo(r, c, label, key, values, default=""):
            tk.Label(grid, text=label, bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                     font=(FONT,9,"bold")).grid(row=r*2, column=c, sticky="w",
                                                 padx=(0,8), pady=(6,2))
            v = tk.StringVar(value=self._initial.get(key, default))
            ttk.Combobox(grid, textvariable=v, values=values, state="readonly"
                         ).grid(row=r*2+1, column=c, sticky="ew", padx=(0,8), pady=(0,4))
            self._vars[key] = v

        add_entry(0, 0, "Título do painel", "titulo")
        add_entry(0, 1, "Fonte", "fonte", default="[Definir]")
        add_entry(1, 0, "Valor em destaque", "valor")
        add_combo(1, 1, "Cor do valor", "valColor",
                  ["var(--navy)","var(--red)","var(--gold-deep)","var(--yellow)",""],
                  default="var(--navy)")
        add_entry(2, 0, "Mês", "mes")
        add_combo(2, 1, "Status do Mês", "mesStatus", STATUS_OPTIONS)
        add_entry(3, 0, "YTD", "ytd")
        add_combo(3, 1, "Status do YTD", "ytdStatus", STATUS_OPTIONS)

        tk.Label(body, text="Gráficos do painel", bg=COLORS["bg"],
                 fg=COLORS["navy"], font=(FONT,10,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(12,4))

        self._charts_editor = ChartsListEditor(body,
                                                value=self._initial.get("charts", []),
                                                kind=kind)
        self._charts_editor.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(0,4))

        if kind == "sac":
            tk.Label(body, text="⚠ O HTML lê os 3 gráficos na ordem acima "
                                "(1º à esquerda, 2º e 3º empilhados à direita).",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gold_deep"],
                     font=(FONT,8,"italic"), justify="left", wraplength=720
                     ).pack(anchor="w")
        else:
            tk.Label(body, text="ℹ Os gráficos aparecem lado a lado. Ordem define "
                                "posição no painel.",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                     font=(FONT,8,"italic")).pack(anchor="w")

        fit_window_to_screen(self, 880, 700, parent=parent, min_w=640, min_h=480)
        enable_minimize_maximize(self, 640, 480, fit_to_content=False)
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda e: self.destroy())

    def _save(self):
        result = deepcopy(self._initial)
        for k, v in self._vars.items():
            result[k] = v.get().strip() if isinstance(v, tk.StringVar) else v
        result["charts"] = self._charts_editor.get_value()

        if not result.get("titulo"):
            messagebox.showerror("Erro", "Informe o título do painel.", parent=self); return
        charts = result["charts"]
        if self._kind == "sac":
            if len(charts) != 3:
                messagebox.showerror("Erro",
                    f"Painel de SAC exige exatamente 3 gráficos (tem {len(charts)}).",
                    parent=self); return
        else:
            if len(charts) < 1:
                messagebox.showerror("Erro", "Adicione pelo menos 1 gráfico.", parent=self); return
            if len(charts) > 6:
                messagebox.showerror("Erro", "Máximo 6 gráficos por painel.", parent=self); return
        for i, c in enumerate(charts, 1):
            if not (c.get("title") or "").strip():
                messagebox.showerror("Erro", f"Gráfico #{i}: informe o título.", parent=self); return

        self.result = result; self.destroy()

# ============================================================
# APP PRINCIPAL
# ============================================================
class DashboardManager(tk.Tk):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.title("Gerenciador do Painel — MBS Brasil")
        self.grid_rowconfigure(2, weight=1)
        self.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.geometry("1420x880")
        self.minsize(1100, 680)
        setup_theme(self)
        self.html_text=""; self.html_range=None; self.html_path=None
        self.data=deepcopy(DEFAULT_DATA)
        self.current_view = CONSOLIDATED_VIEW
        self.dirty=False
        self.history=[]; self.hist_idx=-1
        self._meta_debounce_id=None
        self._ui_debounce_id=None      # ⭐ NOVO — debounce do _update_ui_state
        self._suspend_meta_trace=False
        self._build_ui()
        self._bind_shortcuts()
        self._auto_load()
        self._push_history(initial=True)
        self._update_ui_state()
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.on_close)
        logging.info("Aplicativo iniciado")

    def _push_history(self, initial=False):
        snap=deepcopy(self.data)
        if not initial and self.hist_idx>=0 and self.history[self.hist_idx]==snap: return
        if self.hist_idx < len(self.history)-1:
            self.history=self.history[:self.hist_idx+1]
        self.history.append(snap); self.hist_idx+=1
        if len(self.history)>MAX_HISTORY:
            self.history.pop(0); self.hist_idx-=1

    def _undo(self):
        if self.hist_idx<=0:
            Toast(self,"Nada para desfazer","info"); return
        self.hist_idx-=1; self.data=deepcopy(self.history[self.hist_idx])
        self.refresh_all(); self._mark_dirty("Desfeito (Ctrl+Y p/ refazer)")

    def _redo(self):
        if self.hist_idx>=len(self.history)-1:
            Toast(self,"Nada para refazer","info"); return
        self.hist_idx+=1; self.data=deepcopy(self.history[self.hist_idx])
        self.refresh_all(); self._mark_dirty("Refeito")

    def _on_view_change(self):
        nova = self._view_var.get()
        if nova == self.current_view:
            return
        self.current_view = nova
        self.refresh_all()
        self._mark_clean()
        self.set_status(f"Visão alterada para {nova}")

    def _default_pais(self):
        v = self.current_view
        if v == CONSOLIDATED_VIEW:
            return "ambos"
        return v

    def _visible(self, item):
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            return item
        if not _item_has_multicountry(item):
            return item
        out = dict(item)
        view_vals = _as_dict(item.get("valores", {}).get(self.current_view))
        out.update(view_vals)
        return out

    def _visible_list(self, seq):
        return [self._visible(it) for it in _as_list(seq)]

    def _mark_dirty(self, msg=None):
        self.dirty=True; self._update_ui_state()
        if msg: Toast(self, msg, "info")

    def _mark_clean(self):
        self.dirty=False; self._update_ui_state()

    def _update_ui_state(self):
        base="Gerenciador do Painel — MBS Brasil"
        titulo = f"{base}   [{self.current_view}]"
        self.title(("* " if self.dirty else "") + titulo)
        try:
            self.btn_undo.config(state="normal" if self.hist_idx>0 else "disabled")
            self.btn_redo.config(state="normal" if self.hist_idx<len(self.history)-1 else "disabled")
        except AttributeError: pass
        analysis=Validator(self.data).analyze()
        badge={"ok":"✓","warn":"⚠","error":"✕"}
        names={"config":"⚙  Configurações","descricoes":"📰 Descrições",
               "volumetria":"📊 Volumetria","estrategicos":"🎯 Estratégicos",
               "taticos":"⚡ Táticos","operacionais":"🏭 Operacionais",
               "exportacao":"🌐 Exportação","sac":"🎧 SAC","calculados":"🧮 Calculados"}
        for i,key in enumerate(Validator.TAB_ORDER):
            st=analysis["status"][key]
            self.nb.tab(i, text=f"{badge[st]} {names[key]}")
        total=len(Validator.TAB_ORDER)
        done=sum(1 for k in Validator.TAB_ORDER if analysis["status"][k]=="ok")
        pct=int(done/total*100)
        self.progress_var.set(pct)
        self.progress_lbl.config(text=f"{done}/{total} seções")
        if analysis["errors"]:
            self.lbl_pend.config(text=f"✕ {len(analysis['errors'])} erro(s) crítico(s)",
                                 fg=COLORS["red"])
        elif analysis["pendencies"]:
            self.lbl_pend.config(text=f"⚠ {len(analysis['pendencies'])} pendência(s)",
                                 fg=COLORS["gold_deep"])
        else:
            self.lbl_pend.config(text="✓ Pronto para publicar", fg=COLORS["green"])

    # ⭐ NOVO — debounce do _update_ui_state
    def _schedule_ui_state_update(self):
        if self._ui_debounce_id:
            try: self.after_cancel(self._ui_debounce_id)
            except Exception: pass
        self._ui_debounce_id = self.after(UI_DEBOUNCE_MS, self._flush_ui_state)

    def _flush_ui_state(self):
        self._ui_debounce_id = None
        self._update_ui_state()

    def _build_ui(self):
        header=tk.Frame(self, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=64)
        header.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="ew")
        header.grid_propagate(False)
        left=tk.Frame(header, bg=COLORS["navy"]); left.pack(side="left", padx=(18,8), pady=10)
        tk.Label(left, text="GERENCIADOR", bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,14,"bold")).pack(side="left")
        tk.Label(left, text=" | MBS BRASIL", bg=COLORS["navy"], fg=COLORS["gold"],
                 font=(FONT,11,"bold")).pack(side="left")

        view_box = tk.Frame(header, bg=COLORS["navy_soft"], padx=6, pady=4)
        view_box.pack(side="right", padx=(8,0), pady=12)
        tk.Label(view_box, text="VISÃO:", bg=COLORS["navy_soft"], fg=COLORS["gold"],
                 font=(FONT,8,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=(0,6))
        self._view_var = tk.StringVar(value=self.current_view)
        self._view_buttons = {}
        for v in VIEW_OPTIONS:
            rb = tk.Radiobutton(
                view_box, text=v, value=v, variable=self._view_var,
                indicatoron=False, command=self._on_view_change,
                bg=COLORS["navy_soft"], fg="white",
                selectcolor=COLORS["gold"], activebackground=COLORS["gold"],
                activeforeground=COLORS["navy"], font=(FONT,9,"bold"),
                borderwidth=0, padx=10, pady=2, cursor="hand2",
            )
            rb.pack(side="left", padx=1)
            self._view_buttons[v] = rb

        tb=tk.Frame(header, bg=COLORS["navy"]); tb.pack(side="right", padx=14, pady=10)
        self.btn_undo=ttk.Button(tb, text="↶", width=3, command=self._undo); self.btn_undo.pack(side="left", padx=2)
        self.btn_redo=ttk.Button(tb, text="↷", width=3, command=self._redo); self.btn_redo.pack(side="left", padx=2)
        tk.Frame(tb, bg=COLORS["navy_soft"], width=1).pack(side="left", fill="y", padx=6)
        ttk.Button(tb, text="📂 Importar HTML", style="Gold.TButton",
                   command=self.import_html).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        ttk.Button(tb, text="📥 JSON", command=self.import_json).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        ttk.Button(tb, text="📤 JSON", command=self.export_json).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        tk.Frame(tb, bg=COLORS["navy_soft"], width=1).pack(side="left", fill="y", padx=6)
        ttk.Button(tb, text="✓ Validar (F5)", command=self.show_validation).pack(side="left", padx=3)
        self.btn_publish=ttk.Button(tb, text="💾 Publicar no painel.html", style="Green.TButton",
                                    command=self.save_to_html)
        self.btn_publish.pack(side="left", padx=(6,0))

        bar=tk.Frame(self, bg="#dde3ed", height=32)
        bar.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        bar.grid_propagate(False)
        tk.Label(bar, text="Progresso:", bg="#dde3ed", fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(side="left", padx=(14,6))
        self.progress_var=tk.IntVar(value=0)
        ttk.Progressbar(bar, orient="horizontal", length=240, mode="determinate",
                        variable=self.progress_var).pack(side="left", pady=8)
        self.progress_lbl=tk.Label(bar, text="0/8 seções", bg="#dde3ed",
                                   fg=COLORS["gray_dark"], font=(FONT,9))
        self.progress_lbl.pack(side="left", padx=8)
        self.lbl_pend=tk.Label(bar, text="", bg="#dde3ed", fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                               font=(FONT,9,"bold"))
        self.lbl_pend.pack(side="right", padx=14)

        self.nb=ttk.Notebook(self)
        self.nb.grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="nsew", padx=12, pady=(10,4))
        self._tab_config(); self._tab_descricoes(); self._tab_volumetria()
        self._tab_estrategicos(); self._tab_taticos(); self._tab_operacionais()
        self._tab_exportacao(); self._tab_sac(); self._tab_resumo()
        self._tab_calculados()
        self.status=tk.Label(self, text="Pronto.", bg=COLORS["bg"],
                             fg=COLORS["text_gray"], font=(FONT,9), anchor="w", padx=14)
        self.status.grid(row=3, column=0, sticky="ew")

    def _bind_shortcuts(self):
        self.bind("<Control-z>", lambda e: self._undo())
        self.bind("<Control-y>", lambda e: self._redo())
        self.bind("<Control-Shift-Z>", lambda e: self._redo())
        self.bind("<Control-s>", lambda e: self.save_to_html())
        self.bind("<F5>", lambda e: self.show_validation())
        enable_fullscreen(self)

    def set_status(self, msg, toast=False, kind="info"):
        self.status.config(text=msg)
        if toast: Toast(self, msg, kind)

    def _auto_load(self):
        loaded = False
        if PANEL_HTML.exists():
            try:
                data, html, rng = import_panel_html(PANEL_HTML)
                self.data=data; self.html_text=html; self.html_range=rng
                self.html_path=PANEL_HTML
                self.set_status(f"✓ Carregado de {PANEL_HTML.name}")
                loaded = True
            except Exception as e:
                logging.exception("Falha ao carregar HTML automaticamente")
                self.set_status(f"Falha ao carregar HTML: {e}")

        if not loaded and DATA_JSON.exists():
            try:
                with open(DATA_JSON,"r",encoding="utf-8") as f: self.data=json.load(f)
                for k,v in DEFAULT_DATA.items(): self.data.setdefault(k,deepcopy(v))
                self.set_status("Carregado do backup JSON.")
            except Exception as e:
                logging.exception("Falha ao carregar backup JSON")
                self.set_status(f"Erro backup: {e}")

        migrados = migrate_panels(self.data)
        migrados_mc = migrate_to_multicountry(self.data)
        _migrate_grade_meta_key(self.data)  # ← adicione esta linha
        if migrados_mc > 0:
            corrigidos_pais = _fix_default_pais(self.data)
        else:
            corrigidos_pais = 0

        auto_filled = self._auto_fill_meta_if_needed()
        migrou_grade = self._migrate_taticos_para_grade()
        self.refresh_all()
        self._mark_clean()

        if corrigidos_pais > 0:
            Toast(self,
                  f"ℹ  {corrigidos_pais} card(s) ajustado(s) para 'ambos' (BR+PY).",
                  "info", 4000)

        if migrados > 0:
            Toast(self,
                  f"ℹ  {migrados} painel(is) de destaque migrado(s) para o "
                  f"formato com múltiplos gráficos.",
                  "info", 5000)

        if migrou_grade > 0:
            Toast(self,
                  "Estrutura simplificada ativada — repreencha a grade tática.",
                  "info", 5000)

        if loaded and auto_filled:
            Toast(self, "Período e Atualização preenchidos com a data de hoje.",
                  "info", 4000)
        elif loaded and migrados == 0:
            Toast(self, f"✓ Carregado de {PANEL_HTML.name}", "success")

    def _auto_fill_meta_if_needed(self):
        changed = False
        agora = datetime.now()
        if not periodo_valido(self.data.get("periodo","")):
            self.data["periodo"] = format_periodo(agora); changed = True
        if not atualizacao_valida(self.data.get("atualizacao","")):
            self.data["atualizacao"] = format_atualizacao(agora); changed = True
        return changed

    def _migrate_taticos_para_grade(self):
        if self.data.get("taticos_grade"):
            return 0
        self.data["taticos_grade"] = {
            "BR": {s: {"metas": {}} for s in SETORES_POR_PAIS["BR"]},
            "PY": {s: {"metas": {}} for s in SETORES_POR_PAIS["PY"]},
        }
        return 1

    def import_html(self):
        if self.dirty and not messagebox.askyesno("Descartar alterações?",
                "Há alterações não publicadas. Descartar?"): return
        path=filedialog.askopenfilename(title="Selecione o painel.html",
                                        initialdir=str(BASE_DIR),
                                        filetypes=[("HTML","*.html")])
        if not path: return
        try:
            data, html, rng = import_panel_html(Path(path))
            _migrate_grade_meta_key(data)  # ← adicione esta linha
            self.data=data; self.html_text=html; self.html_range=rng
            self.html_path=Path(path)
            self.history=[]; self.hist_idx=-1
            self._push_history(initial=True)
            self.refresh_all(); self._mark_clean()
            Toast(self, f"Importado: {Path(path).name}", "success")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.exception("Falha ao importar HTML manualmente")
            messagebox.showerror("Erro ao importar HTML", str(e))

    # ⭐ NOVO — pipeline de publicação unificado
    def _build_publish_payload(self):
        self._auto_refresh_atualizacao()
        compilar_taticos_e_estrategicos(self.data)
        self._materialize_calculated_cards()
        _materialize_consolidated(self.data)
        denormalize_panels_for_output(self.data)
        _strip_base_data_for_publish(self.data)
        _drop_empty_kpi_extras(self.data)
        out = _strip_internal(self.data)

        # ⭐ CORREÇÃO — remove itens operacionais sem dados do HTML
        removidos = _strip_empty_operational_items(out)
        self._empty_op_removed_count = removidos
        return out

    def save_to_html(self):
        if not self.html_path or not self.html_path.exists():
            if not PANEL_HTML.exists():
                messagebox.showerror("Sem HTML base",
                                     "Nenhum painel.html carregado. Use 'Importar HTML' primeiro.")
                return
            self.import_html()
            if not self.html_path:
                return

        try:
            with open(self.html_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                fresh_html = f.read()
        except Exception as e:
            logging.exception("Falha ao reler HTML do disco")
            messagebox.showerror("Erro ao ler painel.html", str(e))
            return

        fresh_rng = find_js_object_range(fresh_html, "DATA")
        if not fresh_rng:
            messagebox.showerror("Bloco DATA não encontrado",
                                 "O painel.html foi alterado manualmente e o bloco "
                                 "'var DATA = {...}' não foi encontrado.\n\n"
                                 "Restaure o arquivo ou use 'Importar HTML'.")
            return

        html_mudou = (self.html_text and fresh_html != self.html_text)
        if html_mudou:
            Toast(self,
                  "Alterações manuais no HTML detectadas e preservadas.",
                  "info", 3500)
            logging.info("HTML alterado externamente desde o último "
                         "carregamento; relendo do disco antes de publicar.")

        self.html_text = fresh_html
        self.html_range = fresh_rng

        # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        # Pipeline de publicação — UMA passada só
        # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        data_out = self._build_publish_payload()

        analysis = Validator(self.data).analyze()
        if not self._confirm_publish(analysis):
            return

        self._sync_meta_from_ui()

        bk = None
        if self.html_path.exists():
            bk = backup_current(self.html_path)
            if bk is None:
                proceed = messagebox.askyesno(
                    "Falha ao criar backup",
                    "Não foi possível criar cópia de segurança antes "
                    "de publicar (detalhes em gerenciador.log).\n\n"
                    "Publicar mesmo assim, sem rede de segurança?")
                if not proceed:
                    self.set_status("Publicação cancelada — falha no backup.",
                                    toast=True, kind="warning")
                    return

        removidos = getattr(self, "_empty_op_removed_count", 0)
        if removidos:
            Toast(self,
                  f"🧹 {removidos} item(ns) operacional(is) sem dados "
                  f"foram removidos do HTML.",
                  "info", 5000)

        try:
            export_panel_html(self.html_path, data_out,
                              self.html_text, self.html_range)

            data_reloaded, html, rng = import_panel_html(self.html_path)
            divergiu = (data_reloaded != data_out)
            self.html_text = html
            self.html_range = rng

            with open(DATA_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data_out, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

            self._mark_clean()
            msg = f"✓ Publicado em {self.html_path.name}"
            if bk:
                msg += f"  •  Backup: backups/{bk.name}"
            Toast(self, "Painel publicado com sucesso!", "success", 4000)
            if divergiu:
                Toast(self,
                      "Aviso: dados no disco diferem do cache "
                      "(normalizações do parser).",
                      "warning", 5000)
            self.set_status(msg)
            logging.info("Publicado com sucesso em %s (backup=%s)",
                         self.html_path, bk)
        except Exception as e:
            logging.exception("Falha ao publicar")
            messagebox.showerror("Erro ao publicar", str(e))

    def _auto_refresh_atualizacao(self):
        if atualizacao_valida(self.data.get("atualizacao","")):
            novo = format_atualizacao()
            if novo != self.data["atualizacao"]:
                self.data["atualizacao"] = novo
                try: self.meta_atualizacao.set(novo)
                except Exception: pass

    def _confirm_publish(self, analysis):
        dlg=tk.Toplevel(self); dlg.title("Checklist de publicação")
        dlg.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"]); dlg.transient(self); dlg.grab_set()

        result={"ok":False}
        def on_confirm(): result["ok"]=True; dlg.destroy()
        btns=tk.Frame(dlg, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        _pack_botoes_no_fundo(dlg, btns, padx=18, pady=14)
        ttk.Button(btns, text="Cancelar", command=dlg.destroy).pack(side="right", padx=(8,0))
        if analysis["errors"]:
            tk.Label(btns, text="✕ Corrija os erros críticos para publicar",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["red"],
                     font=(FONT,9,"bold")).pack(side="left")
        elif analysis["pendencies"]:
            ttk.Button(btns, text="⚠ Publicar mesmo assim", style="Gold.TButton",
                       command=on_confirm).pack(side="right")
            tk.Label(btns, text=f"{len(analysis['pendencies'])} pendência(s) — recomendado corrigir",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gold_deep"],
                     font=(FONT,9,"italic")).pack(side="left")
        else:
            ttk.Button(btns, text="✓ Publicar", style="Green.TButton",
                       command=on_confirm).pack(side="right")
            tk.Label(btns, text="Tudo pronto para publicar!",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["green"],
                     font=(FONT,9,"italic")).pack(side="left")

        tk.Frame(dlg, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=48).pack(fill="x", side="top")
        tk.Label(dlg, text="Checklist de publicação", bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,13,"bold")).place(x=18, y=13)

        body_wrap = tk.Frame(dlg, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        body_wrap.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        canvas = tk.Canvas(body_wrap, bg=COLORS["bg"], highlightthickness=0)
        vsb = ttk.Scrollbar(body_wrap, orient="vertical", command=canvas.yview)
        canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=vsb.set)
        vsb.pack(side="right", fill="y"); canvas.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
        inner = tk.Frame(canvas, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        inner_id = canvas.create_window((0, 0), window=inner, anchor="nw")

        def _on_inner_config(_e=None):
            canvas.configure(scrollregion=canvas.bbox("all"))
            canvas.itemconfigure(inner_id, width=canvas.winfo_width())
        inner.bind("<Configure>", _on_inner_config)
        canvas.bind("<Configure>", _on_inner_config)

        body = tk.Frame(inner, bg=COLORS["bg"])
        body.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=18, pady=14)

        tk.Label(body, text="Status das seções", bg=COLORS["bg"],
                 fg=COLORS["navy"], font=(FONT,11,"bold")).pack(anchor="w")
        grid=tk.Frame(body, bg=COLORS["bg"]); grid.pack(fill="x", pady=(4,10))
        names={"config":"Configurações","descricoes":"Descrições","volumetria":"Volumetria",
               "estrategicos":"Estratégicos","taticos":"Táticos","operacionais":"Operacionais",
               "exportacao":"Exportação","sac":"SAC","calculados":"Calculados"}
        for i,key in enumerate(Validator.TAB_ORDER):
            st=analysis["status"][key]
            icon={"ok":"✓","warn":"⚠","error":"✕"}[st]
            col={"ok":COLORS["green"],"warn":COLORS["gold_deep"],"error":COLORS["red"]}[st]
            row,col_i=divmod(i,2)
            fr=tk.Frame(grid, bg=COLORS["bg"]); fr.grid(row=row,column=col_i,sticky="w",padx=4,pady=2)
            tk.Label(fr, text=icon, bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=col,
                     font=(FONT,11,"bold")).pack(side="left")
            tk.Label(fr, text=" "+names[key], bg=COLORS["bg"],
                     fg=COLORS["text"], font=(FONT,10)).pack(side="left")

        if analysis["errors"]:
            tk.Label(body, text="✕ Erros críticos (bloqueiam publicação)",
                     bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["red"],
                     font=(FONT,10,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(8,2))
            for e in analysis["errors"]:
                tk.Label(body, text="   • "+e, bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["red"],
                         font=(FONT,9), anchor="w", justify="left",
                         wraplength=560).pack(anchor="w")
        if analysis["pendencies"]:
            tk.Label(body, text="⚠ Pendências (avisos)", bg=COLORS["bg"],
                     fg=COLORS["gold_deep"], font=(FONT,10,"bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(8,2))
            for p in analysis["pendencies"][:6]:
                tk.Label(body, text="   • "+p, bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                         font=(FONT,9), anchor="w", justify="left",
                         wraplength=560).pack(anchor="w")
            if len(analysis["pendencies"])>6:
                tk.Label(body, text=f"   ... e mais {len(analysis['pendencies'])-6}",
                         bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["text_gray"],
                         font=(FONT,8,"italic")).pack(anchor="w")

        fit_window_to_screen(dlg, 620, 560, parent=self, min_w=520, min_h=420)
        enable_minimize_maximize(dlg, 520, 420, fit_to_content=False)
        enable_fullscreen(dlg)

        self.wait_window(dlg)
        return result["ok"]

    def import_json(self):
        path=filedialog.askopenfilename(filetypes=[("JSON","*.json")])
        if not path: return
        try:
            with open(path,"r",encoding="utf-8") as f: self.data=json.load(f)
            for k,v in DEFAULT_DATA.items(): self.data.setdefault(k,deepcopy(v))
            _ensure_section_defaults(self.data)
            self.refresh_all(); self._push_history()
            Toast(self, f"Importado: {Path(path).name}", "success")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.exception("Falha ao importar JSON")
            messagebox.showerror("Erro", str(e))

    def export_json(self):
        path = filedialog.asksaveasfilename(
            defaultextension=".json",
            initialfile="dashboard_data.json",
            filetypes=[("JSON", "*.json")])
        if not path:
            return
        try:
            # Reaproveita o pipeline de publicação — mesma ordem,
            # mesmo resultado, sem risco de divergência.
            self._auto_refresh_atualizacao()
            compilar_taticos_e_estrategicos(self.data)
            self._materialize_calculated_cards()
            _materialize_consolidated(self.data)
            denormalize_panels_for_output(self.data)
            _strip_base_data_for_publish(self.data)
            _drop_empty_kpi_extras(self.data)  # ← adicione
            data_out = _strip_internal(self.data)
            _strip_empty_operational_items(data_out)

            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data_out, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

            Toast(self, f"Exportado: {Path(path).name}", "success")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.exception("Falha ao exportar JSON")
            messagebox.showerror("Erro", str(e))

    def show_validation(self):

        dlg=tk.Toplevel(self); dlg.title("Validação"); dlg.configure(bg=COLORS["bg"])

        btns = tk.Frame(dlg, bg=COLORS["bg"])

        tk.Frame(dlg, bg=COLORS["navy"], height=44).pack(fill="x", side="top")
        tk.Label(dlg, text="Validação do painel", bg=COLORS["navy"], fg="white",
                 font=(FONT,12,"bold")).place(x=18, y=12)

        body_wrap = tk.Frame(dlg, bg=COLORS["bg"])
