        if dlg.result:
            self.data["sac"]["kpis"][i]={**self.data["sac"]["kpis"][i], **dlg.result}
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_sac(); self._mark_dirty("KPI editado")

    def _sac_kpi_dup(self):
        s=self.sac_kpi.selection()
        if not s: return
        i=int(s[0]); c=deepcopy(self.data["sac"]["kpis"][i])
        c["titulo"]=(c.get("titulo","")+" (cópia)").strip()
        self.data["sac"]["kpis"].insert(i+1,c)
        self._push_history(); self._refresh_sac(); self._mark_dirty("KPI duplicado")

    def _sac_kpi_del(self):
        s=self.sac_kpi.selection()
        if not s: return
        if messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar","Excluir KPI?"):
            for iid in sorted([int(x) for x in s], reverse=True):

    def _sac_panel_add(self):
        if self.data["sac"].get("paineisDestaque"):
            messagebox.showwarning("Já existe um painel",
                "O HTML do SAC lê apenas o primeiro item de 'paineisDestaque'. "
                "Edite o painel existente em vez de adicionar um segundo.")
            return
        dlg = PainelEditorDialog(self, "Novo Painel de SAC", kind="sac")
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result:
            dlg.result.setdefault("pais", self._default_pais())
            if not self._validate_unique_label(dlg.result, "titulo"):
                return
            self.data.setdefault("sac", {"kpis": [], "paineisDestaque": []})
            self.data["sac"].setdefault("paineisDestaque", []).append(dlg.result)
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_sac(); self._mark_dirty("Painel adicionado")

    def _sac_panel_edit(self):
        s = self.sac_panel.selection()
        if not s: return
        i = int(s[0])
        p = self.data["sac"]["paineisDestaque"][i]
        dlg = PainelEditorDialog(self, "Editar Painel de SAC",
                                  initial=p, kind="sac")
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result:
            if not self._validate_unique_label(
                    dlg.result, "titulo",
                    ignore_path=("sac", "paineisDestaque", i)):
                return
            self.data["sac"]["paineisDestaque"][i] = dlg.result
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_sac(); self._mark_dirty("Painel editado")

    def _sac_panel_del(self):
        s = self.sac_panel.selection()
        if not s: return
        if messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar",
                "Excluir painel? Os gráficos de SAC não serão exibidos até cadastrar um novo."):
            for iid in sorted([int(x) for x in s], reverse=True):
                try: del self.data["sac"]["paineisDestaque"][iid]
                except (IndexError, KeyError): pass
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_sac(); self._mark_dirty("Painel excluído")

    # ========================================================
    # ABA RESUMO
    # ========================================================
    def _tab_resumo(self):
        f=ttk.Frame(self.nb, padding=14); self.nb.add(f, text="📈 Resumo")
        top=tk.Frame(f, bg=COLORS["bg"]); top.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,8))
        ttk.Button(top, text="🔄 Recalcular", style="Primary.TButton",
                   command=self._refresh_resumo).pack(side="left")
        self.resumo_lbl_total=tk.Label(f, text="", bg=COLORS["bg"],
                                       font=(FONT,9,"italic"), fg=COLORS["text_gray"])
        self.resumo_lbl_total.pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,8))

        row=tk.Frame(f, bg=COLORS["bg"]); row.pack(fill="x", pady=(0,10))
        specs=[("green",TIER_LABEL["green"],COLORS["green"]),
               ("yellow",TIER_LABEL["yellow"],COLORS["gold_deep"]),
               ("red",TIER_LABEL["red"],COLORS["red"]),
               ("gray",TIER_LABEL["gray"],COLORS["text_gray"])]
        self.resumo_count_labels={}
        for key,label,color in specs:
            card=tk.Frame(row, bg=COLORS["card"], highlightthickness=1,
                          highlightbackground=COLORS["border"])
            card.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True, padx=(0,8))
            lbl_n=tk.Label(card, text="0", bg=COLORS["card"], fg=color,
                           font=(FONT,22,"bold"))
            lbl_n.pack(pady=(12,0))
            tk.Label(card, text=label, bg=COLORS["card"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                     font=(FONT,9)).pack(pady=(0,12))
            self.resumo_count_labels[key]=lbl_n

        make_card(f, "Indicadores que precisam de atenção (vermelho primeiro)")
        self.resumo_tree=FilterableTree(f, [
            ("secao","Seção",160),("indicador","Indicador",420),
            ("percentual","% da meta",100),("status","Status",140)])
        self.resumo_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

    def _refresh_resumo(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "resumo_count_labels"): return
        counts, atencao, total = Insights.summarize(self.data)
        for key,lbl in self.resumo_count_labels.items():
            lbl.config(text=str(counts.get(key,0)))
        self.resumo_lbl_total.config(
            text=f"{total} indicador(es) com meta numérica analisado(s) — "
                 f"Estratégicos + Operacionais + Táticos. Aproximação de 3 faixas.")
        rows=[]
        for idx,(sec,label,tier,p) in enumerate(atencao):
            pct=f"{Calc.format_br(p)}%" if p is not None else "—"
            rows.append((str(idx),(sec, label, pct, TIER_LABEL.get(tier,tier))))
        self.resumo_tree.set_rows(rows)

    # ========================================================
    # ABA CALCULADOS
    # ========================================================
    def _tab_calculados(self):
        f=ttk.Frame(self.nb, padding=14); self.nb.add(f, text="🧮 Calculados")
        toolbar(f, [
            ("➕ Adicionar", "Primary.TButton", self._calc_add),
            ("✏ Editar",    None,               self._calc_edit),
            ("📋 Duplicar", None,               self._calc_dup),
            ("🗑 Excluir",  "Danger.TButton",   self._calc_del),
            ("🧮 Testar todas", None,           self._calc_test_all),
            ("🔍 Verificar duplicatas", None,   self._scan_duplicates),
        ])
        tk.Label(f,
                 text="Use  [Rótulo do card]  para referenciar qualquer valor "
                      "(Volumetria, Estratégicos, Operacionais, Táticos ou outro Calculado). "
                      "Operadores: + - * / % ^  ( ). Funções: min, max, abs, round.",
                 bg=COLORS["bg"], fg=COLORS["gray_dark"],
                 font=(FONT,9,"italic"), justify="left", wraplength=1200
                 ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0,6))
        self.calc_tree = FilterableTree(f, [
            ("label",   "Rótulo",          240),
            ("formula", "Fórmula",         420),
            ("format",  "Formato",         140),
            ("value",   "Valor calculado", 140),
            ("status",  "Status",          200),
        ], on_double=lambda e: self._calc_edit())
        self.calc_tree.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.calc_tree.tree.bind("<Delete>", lambda e: self._calc_del())

    def _refresh_calc(self):
        if not hasattr(self, "calc_tree"): return
        rows=[]
        registry = build_formula_registry(self.data)
        for i, c in enumerate(_as_list(self.data.get("calculados"))):
            lbl     = c.get("label","")
            formula = c.get("formula","")
            fmt     = c.get("format","decimal")
            dec     = c.get("decimals",2)
            value, err = Formula.evaluate(formula, registry)
            c["value"] = value; c["_error"] = err
            if err:
                display, status = "—", "⚠ " + err
            elif value is None:
                display, status = "—", "⚠ sem valor"
            else:
                display = Formatters.format(value, fmt, dec)
                status  = "✓ OK"
            rows.append((str(i), (lbl, formula, fmt, display, status)))
        self.calc_tree.set_rows(rows)

    def _calc_fields(self):
        return [
            {"key":"label",   "label":"Rótulo (como será referenciado nas fórmulas)", "type":"text"},
            {"key": "formula",
             "label": "Fórmula  —  clique em '📋 Inserir CARD' para escolher entre os cards disponíveis",
             "type": "formula_calc"},
            {"key":"format",  "label":"Formatação do resultado", "type":"combo",
             "values":[k for k,_ in Formatters.OPTIONS],
             "default":"decimal"},
            {"key":"decimals","label":"Casas decimais (só p/ 'decimal', 'percent', 'currency')",
             "type":"int", "default":2},
            {"key":"icon",    "label":"Ícone do card", "type":"combo", "values":ICONS,
             "default":"calculator"},
            {"key":"color",   "label":"Cor do card", "type":"combo", "values":COLORS_OPTIONS,
             "default":"navy"},
            {"key":"fonte",   "label":"Fonte / observação", "type":"text", "default":"Cálculo interno"},
        ]

    def _calc_preview(self, raw):
        formula = (raw.get("formula") or "").strip()
        if not formula:
            return ("Digite uma fórmula. Ex.: [Card A] + [Card B] * 1.15", COLORS["text_gray"])
        registry = build_formula_registry(self.data)
        value, err = Formula.evaluate(formula, registry)
        if err:
            return (f"⚠ {err}", COLORS["red"])
        if value is None:
            return ("⚠ Valor indefinido", COLORS["red"])
        try: dec = int(raw.get("decimals") or 2)
        except (TypeError, ValueError): dec = 2
        display = Formatters.format(value, raw.get("format","decimal"), dec)
        return (f"✓  {display}   (valor bruto: {value:.4f})", COLORS["green"])

    def _calc_add(self):
        dlg=FormDialog(self, "Novo Card Calculado", self._calc_fields(),
                       preview_fn=self._calc_preview,
                       cards_provider=lambda: list_available_cards(self.data))
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result:
            dlg.result.setdefault("pais", self._default_pais())
            if not self._validate_unique_label(dlg.result, "label"):
                return
            self.data.setdefault("calculados", []).append(dlg.result)
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_calc(); self._mark_dirty("Card calculado adicionado")

    def _calc_edit(self):
        s=self.calc_tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        i=int(s[0]); item=self.data["calculados"][i]
        dlg=FormDialog(self, "Editar Card Calculado", self._calc_fields(),
                       initial=item, preview_fn=self._calc_preview,
                       cards_provider=lambda: list_available_cards(self.data))
        self.wait_window(dlg)
        if dlg.result:
            merged={**item, **dlg.result}
            if not self._validate_unique_label(merged, "label",
                                                ignore_path=("calculados", i)):
                return
            self.data["calculados"][i]=merged
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_calc(); self._mark_dirty("Card calculado editado")

    def _calc_dup(self):
        s=self.calc_tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        i=int(s[0]); c=deepcopy(self.data["calculados"][i])
        c["label"]=(c.get("label","")+" (cópia)").strip()
        self.data["calculados"].insert(i+1, c)
        self._push_history(); self._refresh_calc(); self._mark_dirty("Card calculado duplicado")

    def _calc_del(self):
        s=self.calc_tree.selection()
        if not s: return
        if messagebox.askyesno("Confirmar","Excluir card calculado?"):
            for iid in sorted([int(x) for x in s], reverse=True):
                try: del self.data["calculados"][iid]
                except (IndexError, KeyError): pass
            self._push_history(); self._refresh_calc(); self._mark_dirty("Card calculado excluído")

    def _calc_test_all(self):
        self._refresh_calc()
        registry = build_formula_registry(self.data)
        linhas=[]
        for c in _as_list(self.data.get("calculados")):
            lbl = c.get("label") or "(sem rótulo)"
            v, err = Formula.evaluate(c.get("formula",""), registry)
            if err: linhas.append(f"⚠ {lbl}: {err}")
            else:
                disp = Formatters.format(v, c.get("format","decimal"), c.get("decimals",2))
                linhas.append(f"✓ {lbl} = {disp}")
        messagebox.showinfo("Teste de fórmulas",
                            "\n".join(linhas) if linhas else "Nenhum card calculado cadastrado.",
                            parent=self)

    def _scan_duplicates(self):
        dups = find_all_duplicate_labels(self.data)
        if not dups:
            messagebox.showinfo("Verificação de rótulos",
                "✓ Nenhum rótulo duplicado encontrado.\n\n"
                "Todos os cards têm nomes únicos, então as fórmulas "
                "resolvem corretamente.", parent=self)
            return
        linhas = [f"⚠ {len(dups)} rótulo(s) duplicado(s):\n"]
        for lbl, locs in dups:
            linhas.append(f"\n[{lbl}]  →  {len(locs)} ocorrências:")
            for loc in locs:
                linhas.append(f"     • {loc}")
        linhas.append("\n\nRenomeie os cards duplicados para que as "
                      "fórmulas os distingam corretamente.")
        messagebox.showwarning("Rótulos duplicados", "\n".join(linhas), parent=self)

    def _materialize_calculated_cards(self):
        self.data["volumetria"] = [
            c for c in _as_list(self.data.get("volumetria"))
            if not c.get("_from_calculated")
        ]

        for view in ("BR", "PY", "BR+PY"):
            registry = build_formula_registry(self.data, view=view)
            for c in _as_list(self.data.get("calculados")):
                lbl = (c.get("label") or "").strip()
                if not lbl:
                    continue
                val, err = Formula.evaluate(c.get("formula", ""), registry)
                c.setdefault("valores", {})
                c["valores"].setdefault(view, {})
                c["valores"][view]["value"] = val
                c["valores"][view]["_error"] = err
                if view == "BR":
                    c["value"] = val
                    c["_error"] = err

        for c in _as_list(self.data.get("calculados")):
            for view in ("BR", "PY", "BR+PY"):
                v = _as_dict(c.get("valores", {}).get(view))
                val = v.get("value")
                err = v.get("_error")
                if err is not None or val is None:
                    continue
                if view != "BR+PY":
                    continue
                display = Formatters.format(val, c.get("format", "decimal"),
                                            c.get("decimals", 2))
                self.data["volumetria"].append({
                    "icon": c.get("icon", "calculator"),
                    "color": c.get("color", "navy"),
                    "label": c.get("label"),
                    "type": "calculated",
                    "value": val,
                    "display": display,
                    "formula": c.get("formula", ""),
                    "fonte": c.get("fonte", "Cálculo interno"),
                    "_from_calculated": True,
                    "pais": "ambos",
                    "valores": c.get("valores", {}),
                })
                break

    def on_close(self):
        if self._meta_debounce_id:
            try: self.after_cancel(self._meta_debounce_id)
            except Exception: pass
        if self._ui_debounce_id:
            try: self.after_cancel(self._ui_debounce_id)
            except Exception: pass
        if self.dirty:
            r=messagebox.askyesnocancel("Sair",
                "Há alterações não publicadas.\n\n"
                "Sim → publicar e sair\nNão → sair sem publicar\nCancelar → voltar")
            if r is None: return
            if r:
                self.save_to_html()
                if self.dirty: return
        else:
            if not messagebox.askyesno("Sair","Deseja sair?"): return
        try:
            with open(DATA_JSON,"w",encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(_strip_internal(self.data),f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)
        except Exception: logging.exception("Falha ao salvar backup JSON ao sair")
        self.destroy()


# ============================================================
if __name__ == "__main__":
    app=DashboardManager()
    app.mainloop()
